## 6. The stats map

### 6.1 What needs ids

*As built (S2, the owner's "A: nothing held", 2026-11-20; section 1).* Nothing here is resident: the ids and the album bits are a console ask's (`Hl`, `Hw`: `app/StatsLists`), made by its walk in its one block and given back at its end. The map holds the asked lists' hashes alone, not RCNT's (no list holds an RCNT row the lists don't). No live play's id is asked: no list is held between asks for one to patch. S3 decides how long a page holds them. Read "Resident" below as "an ask's"; the walk's transients are in the same block.

- Ranked rows and the shelves' snapshots need track ids: the rankers give hashes.
- Not played yet needs, for each album, whether any of its tracks has a play. That needs every played hash.
- The NP sheet needs neither: the playing track's hash is known at its start (`src/app/PlayHistory.cpp:149-152`), and its facts come from its TRKS row (6.8). Its jump uses `kShowPlaying` (`src/ui/Page.h:46`).
- A live play's id comes from `findTrack(p.file.path)` with no walk (from S2: C14).
- Track records hold no hash (`lib/core/LibraryIndex.h:182-195`), so a bare hash needs the walk.

**Resident:**
- a hash → id map for the hashes in RCNT and in the lists, sorted by hash, 12 B a row (u64, u32 id: no 65,534 cap, unlike Auto DJ's u16 maps, `lib/core/AutoDjJoin.h:120-122`);
- one bit an album: played or not.

**Transient, for a walk:**
- every hash with a play, sorted, 8 B each, in chunks of 2,048;
- the folder states.

### 6.2 The walk

- The Folders and Hash phases of Auto DJ's join (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.cpp:584-647`: each folder's path hashed once, then each track's hash from its folder's) go into a portable stepper, `lib/core/PathHashWalk`. The join keeps using it with the same output, so there is one hashing rule, already tested against `trackPathHash()` (`:105-120`; `docs/AUTODJ.md:1499`).
- The stats run it alone, on the loop, in slices of up to 8 ms a pass, as `stepJoin` gives the join (`src/app/AutoDj.cpp:624-649`).
- Its gates: the UI started; the index readable; the card not held; no list moving; no update holding the index; the lists ranked; and never in a pass where `joinWorking()` is true. Two walks in one pass would stretch it from about 13.5 ms to about 21.
- While it works, the loop's idle wait is `kJoinLoopDelayMs` (`src/main.cpp:123`), as for the join (`:4255`).
- For each track it hashes: the hash is looked up in the played set (its album's bit set) and in the wanted set (its id kept).
- It aborts when the index isn't the one it began on (pointer, `ready()`, `trackCount()`, `buildStamp()`), as `Join::matches` does (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.cpp:294-297`). It starts again; it never resumes.
- `Join::setPathHash()` is not the hook: any hook but the default turns off the folder states (`:342`), and the join would hash whole paths again (about twice its CPU).
- Riding the join while Auto DJ is on is left out (14).
- **As built** (C11, C13, C14): `lib/core/PathHashWalk` (`pathhash::Walk`: `foldersSlice()`, `hashSlice()` with a visitor, `trackHash()`, `matches()`; `trackPathHash()` moved there, `autodj::trackPathHash` its name in the join) takes the join's Folders and Hash phases, and the join its Paths' confirms (the walk keeps nothing the join keeps: `autodj::Join` stays 824 B and Auto DJ's object 3,864 B on the device, as on main, so its PSRAM carve at setup keeps its size class: the review); `lib/core/StatsMap` is the walk's consumer (the played set's binary search for the album bits, the wanted set's for the ids; a hash two tracks share maps neither). The firmware walks after Auto DJ's step (`main.cpp`'s `stepStatsMap()`), up to 8 ms a pass, never in a pass `joinWorking()`, with a list moving, the card held or the UI not up; the loop's idle wait is `kJoinLoopDelayMs` meanwhile. It isn't held by an update asked: the update waits for the ask's block (`UpdateEnv::statsWait`), so its fence never comes under the walk. An index that changes under it begins it again, three times, then the ask ends.

### 6.3 Cost

- On the host, the hashing was 1.0 ms of a 5.8-6.1 ms join, and the device's join is 0.55-0.65 s of CPU (`docs/AUTODJ.md:977`). So the hashing is about 0.1 s of device CPU (ESTIMATED).
- With a lookup a track: 0.12-0.2 s of CPU, 15-25 passes of about 13.5 ms; about 0.2-0.4 s idle and 0.4-0.8 s with an MP3 (GUESS). S2 measures it (`Hw`).

### 6.4 When it walks

*Superseded (A, 2026-11-20).* The walk runs when an ask needs it (`Hl`, `Hw`), and never by itself: none after the boot's ranking or a Live, none at a list's new hash, and no cache key, since nothing is kept. The list below is the plan as it was.

- After the boot's ranking.
- After every update's Live (`src/main.cpp:3460-3467`).
- When a list gains a hash with no id that isn't a live play's (a UTC day's change can bring one into a window).
- The cache key is (`buildStamp()`, `trackCount()`) (`lib/core/LibraryIndex.h:467-471`). The index is the same object across a rebuild (`src/app/Library.cpp:106-109`), so the pointer is no key. The stamp leaves out the mood words, so a score-only rebuild keeps the ids.

### 6.5 Sizes at 19,478 tracks

Carves are MODELLED: + 12 B, rounded up to the 1/32 class, + a 4 B header (`test/test_autodj_join/test_autodj_join.cpp:1892, 1910-1916`). Each figure is a block's footprint, its carve plus the header; the request is in brackets. A chunk of 16,384 B takes 16,900 B, and so does S1's 16,392 B chunk of RCNT and TRKD rows (5.6).

| Block | Life | P = 2,214 played | P = 9,739 | P = 19,478 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Played set, 8 B a hash, chunks of 2,048 (16,384 B) | Walk | 2 chunks: 33,800 B | 5: 84,500 B | 10: 169,000 B |
| Folder states, 10 B a folder | Walk | 25,092 B (24,840 B at 2,484 folders) | same | same |
| The rank's snapshot of TRKD and RCNT, chunks of 16,384 B (5.6) | A rank | 1-2 chunks: 16,900-33,800 B | up to 9: 152,100 B | up to 9: 152,100 B |
| Hash → id, 12 B a row (RCNT and lists: at most 4,792 rows), chunks of 16,384 B | Resident | 1 chunk: 16,900 B | up to 4: 67,600 B (57,504 B) | up to 4: 67,600 B |
| Album bits | Resident | 236 B (219 B at 1,747 albums) | same | same |
| Not played yet's list (u32 an album, as FilteredAlbums) | While that shelf shows | 7,044 B (6,988 B) | same | same |
| A shelf's snapshot (200 rows) | While it shows | 3,268 B (about 3.2 KB) | same | same |

- Every block goes through `psramAlloc` (`src/app/Psram.h:10-14`): a malloc under 4 KB would land in internal RAM.
- A dense 4 B x 19,478 array (77,912 B, carving 79,872) is avoided: it is the size class of Auto DJ's filters block, which once failed to place after an update.

*As built (A), MODELLED (`test_an_s2_ask_in_a_model_heap`; the block's head, the firmware's `struct Ask`, taken as 4,608 B).* No column of the table above is resident. An ask carves one block at the top of the largest free block (the hold's caps above it; a decoder's room asked), holding its state and everything its steps fill, and gives it back at its end:
- `Hr`: the rank's work memory (`statsfile::kWorkBytes`), the lists (`Ranker::kBytes`, 16,704 B: each list its cap and 16), RCNT's rows in the 30 days (12 B each, room for 4,096 and the records'), the records' copy (40 x 36 B): **83,872 B** at any P.
- `Hl0`-`Hl2`, `Hl4`: `Hr`'s, the folders' states (24,840 B at 2,484 folders), the album bits and one wanted chunk (16,380 B): about 125 KB.
- `Hw`, `Hl3`: `Hl`'s and the played set (16,384 B a chunk of 2,048 hashes): **158,876 B** at P = 2,214, **289,948 B** at P = 19,478 (ten chunks).
- `Hp`: the head and the work memory, about 15 KB.
- Beside it, two FatFiles (4,148 B each, `stats.bin` and the base), opened on the loop for the worker's step and closed after it.

*As decided (2026-11-20, later; S3: 9.7 has the layouts, the grades and the fit).* The table's two "while it shows" rows become one block, and S3's asks carve smaller blocks than `Hw`'s:
- **The snapshot** replaces "Not played yet's list (u32 an album)" (7,044 B) and "a shelf's snapshot" (3,268 B a shelf). It is one plain `psramAlloc` block for all the stats pages: 3,860 B on the spare (1,706 albums), at most 4,096 B. It is held while on the stats pages. *As decided (2026-11-21, "Borrow Dance's block"):* it is lent the Dance tab's 4,096 B tracker block, which `DanceMode` carves at the boot and keeps, so the stats carve nothing for it: 0 heap bytes (9.7's "S3a: the owner's calls as built").
- **The visit's ask** is one block at the top through the hold, as S2's. Its regions are reused once dead: the Ranker's, the work memory's and the records' room becomes the folders' states, the album bits and the wanted set (8,352 B for the lists' 696 rows); the RCNT window's room becomes the u40 played set. On the spare: **Full** 130,688 B (P = 19,478 with TRKD 1,365: 139,264 B), **Lists** 81,360 B, **Lists without a clock** 34,624 B.
- **The lean Not played yet ask**: 122,336 B (130,912 B), and 109,744 B (118,320 B) without the folders' states.
- **Compared with S2's `Hw`**: 285,836 B on the spare. 9.7 gives the regions' bytes.
- *As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):* the ask's head is 1,600 B, not the estimated 1,488 B, so each ask above is 112 B more: Full 130,800 B (139,376 B), Lists 81,472 B, Lists without a clock 34,736 B, the lean ask 122,448 B (131,024 B), without the folders' states 109,856 B (118,432 B). The snapshot is as above (9.7's "As built (S3a)").

### 6.6 Rebuilds, renames, orphans

- **An update step:** behind the fence `Library::index()` is null (`lib/core/LibraryUpdate.h:109-114`). The hash-keyed data (everything in `stats.bin`) is untouched. The ids, album bits and snapshots are dropped at `enterFence()` (`src/main.cpp:3412-3417`) and their bytes counted in `alsoFreed` (`:3570`). (S3's snapshot since V8: released with no heap bytes, so none counted: 9.7.) The walk runs after Live.
- **A rename, a conversion to Opus, a name in another normal form:** a new path hash. The old row stays in the totals and matches no track: an orphan, in no shelf. Following renames through ORIG's audioHash is later (spec 8.6).
- **A track gone from the card:** an orphan.
- **Two siblings equal under textfold:** `findTrack()` still finds each, since `textfold::compare()` falls back to the bytes (`lib/core/TextFold.cpp:631-632`) and the files are sorted by it (`lib/core/LibraryIndex.cpp:80-95, 1340-1345`).
- **A 64-bit collision:** the walk maps neither, as the join does (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.cpp:805-809`).
- **The `/music/` prefix:** PlayHistory checks it case-sensitively (`src/app/PlayHistory.cpp:150-151`), `trackPathHash()` in any case. They agree because the index's root is the literal `/music` (`lib/core/LibraryUpdate.h:242`).

### 6.7 The heap model

**As built: the rework** (5.14, R1-R7; MEASURED-HOST). `test/test_autodj_join` models the stats with the firmware's own glue (`statsglue::Glue`, its fold and its store) on a card in memory (`test/support/CutFs.h`), every block it carves going through the model heap as the firmware's hooks carve them: no block for the object since the move to internal RAM (2026-11-17; until then at `PlayHistory::begin()`'s place, at its 3,584 B budget, the device's 3,200 B); the boot's read (its work memory and a 4,148 B FatFile for each file open, given back in the call; MARK's block kept for a card past 8 rows, where the model heap puts it since fix G); the scan's ranges' block and the overflow's arena at the top through the spacers keeping 64 KB with the block carved, the free block above each closed by caps (fix A, 5.14's "The hold's T"); each save's work memory (`statsfile::kWorkBytes`) and FatFiles in the store's own order of opens and closes. Nothing of the stats is given back during an update: the memory check's hook is Auto DJ's alone, and the stats add nothing to the fence or to `alsoFreed`. Two cases:
- `test_the_listening_stats_across_updates` (5.14's yardstick, R1): 5 updates of the 19,412-track library, the decoders' long-lived carves as the device asks for them, a `Dc` after each or not, joined per step against main's run in the same history; the rule at each step is the mood plan's (at or over 245,764 B where main is, within 122,880 B of main's), and the boot's join must be main's (main's with the Opus first where a start at the boot opened it before the join). The rework, in ten ways (a usual boot or a full log, the scan or the join first, a records' save at 40 (96 until 2026-11-17) in a fence before or after its build, a boot save failing all four tries, the scan after an update, and the root's ACKS 16 rows above MARK with a usual boot and with a full log: the ranges' block 3,076 B), fails none of the 13 named histories' 65 steps, nor, built with `-DSTATS_WIDE`, of the 360 steps of 72 histories, with the device's decoder requests; with the old shapes (`-DSTATS_OLD_DECODERS`) only the control's (main with its queue block 2,048 B longer). A start asked during a scan that comes first opens the Opus before the boot's join, as the firmware orders it (the rework review), so those ways are held to main's with the Opus first: 0 of 65 steps failing (mean -4,254 B, worst -110,592 B) and, built with -DSTATS_WIDE, 0 of 360 (mean -1,246 B); the join first, 0 of 65 (mean -1,890 B) and 0 of 360 (mean -933 B), R6's; with the old shapes a usual boot's start, held by nothing now, costs the boot's join one class (450,548 B against 466,932 B) where a start comes during its scan: the owner's call (5.14's R6 notes), settled by the move (with the object out of PSRAM that join is main's with the Opus first: section 1). Its waits are load-bearing: a full log's overflow held through update 0's build fails 13 of 65 steps (62 of 360), carved in its fence 1 (25), and the Opus opening under a full log's overflow beside a 3,076 B ranges' block (no start waiting) 10 of 65 and the boot's join 65,536 B under main's with the Opus first; with 1,284 B of ranges it fails none, whether the start comes after the scan's first piece, its 50th or its last (5.14's R6 notes and the rework review's). S1 as first built failed 47 of the 360 and its boot's join (507,892 B against 589,812 B).
- `test_the_listening_stats_in_a_model_heap`: the rework's 13 shapes (P = 2,214 and 19,478, 300 slots and a full log, the scan or the join first, the boot's save landing at its second try or failing all four, the scan after the first update, a card of 12 players' MARK rows), two updates, against the control: the mood plan's rule whole, joined the control's 360,436 B in every shape (within its class, 8,192 B, 5.14's rule), the boot's join 589,812 B, each shape's blocks, tries, saves and compactions pinned.

- **S2's half, as built (A, 2026-11-20; C13).** No id-keyed block is held between asks, so there is none to drop at the fence or take again after a Live. `StatsRun` gains an ask (`StatsRun::Ask`: `Hr`, or `Hw`, the largest block an ask carves) after the boot's join and after each update's rejoin and plays, as the firmware orders it: its block at the top through the hold (`g_modelTop`, the decoder's room asked, yielding), the two FatFiles, then everything given back. `test_an_s2_ask_in_a_model_heap` runs it at P = 2,214 and P = 19,478 against the twin with no ask (MODELLED):
  - every figure of the run is the twin's: the boot's join (589,812 B), the fence's free blocks, joined (360,436 B), given back;
  - every ask leaves free, the largest free block and the free blocks of 64 KB or more as it found them;
  - while held, the largest free block still takes the Opus's buffers (at least 192,500 B) and free stays over the hold's 64 KB (at least 245,688 B);
  - the session's first Opus opening inside an ask's hold finds its room;
  - **`Hw`'s block at P = 19,478 (289,948 B) is refused for room after each library update** (joined 360,436 B in the model; on the device 262-295 KB after an update: MOOD-FILTERS Run D). So is `Hw` at P = 2,214 once the session's first Opus holds its 128 KB. Both are taken at the boot. `Hr`'s 83,872 B fits everywhere.
- Fix A's cases (5.14, "The hold's T", MEASURED-HOST): `test_the_hold_leaves_main_s_layout`'s Y1-Y7 (written first: they fail at fe61db9's hooks and are main's line with the fix), Y5b-Y5d, and U1-U11 and U13-U16 (the caps' rule, the walk's decisions through `autodj::WalkAbove`, the release order, a decoder's room through `statsglue::decoderRoom()`); `test_autodj_join` 45 cases since the final review's second round.

The model doesn't reproduce S1's device round 2 on round 2's own sequence, so the device re-run against main's figures (5.14), not the model, accepts the resident block and the transients' placement. It has since: the move's re-run (2026-11-17) gave main's PSRAM figures to the byte in its sequence, B1 then found the move's hold splitting the largest free block where the first frame or a queue came inside it (a sequence the model didn't have), and fix A's runs (2026-11-18) gave main's figures to the byte at the join and in `D` on B1's I-3m-like and full-log boots (an after-stop split aside, main's own: C3/M3) (5.14's "Its device re-run", "The hold's T" and "Its device runs"), and under #64's 4,095 B limit C4/M4 and visit A (2026-11-19) gave main 987216f's to the byte again, a track's stdio buffer opened inside the hold among them; L, Lq and Lo2 after an I-3m-like boot are not run.

**History: S1 as first built** (the code at 414488c): the C8 design, the ladder, the give-back and their figures, as they stood; the rework removed the resident tables, the give-back and its shapes (5.14).

- `test_a_library_update_in_a_model_heap` (`test/test_autodj_join/test_autodj_join.cpp:2789`) gains a `StatsRun`, beside `MoodRun` (`:2674-2680`), in the order the blocks come on the device:
  - S1 (C8), the hash-keyed half: the load's chunks at `PlayHistory::begin()`'s place, after Auto DJ's glue (`src/main.cpp:3901, 3908`); the boot fold's growth, up to the full-log case (TRKD about 120 KB and its 32 KB index; RCNT, pruned only at the save, about 160 KB in 10 chunks and its 64 KB index: 412,992 B held at the peak in `test_stats_fold`'s full log, 396,744 B since the chunks hold whole rows, TRKD's 4,653 rows in 7 chunks, not 8), after the boot's `joinNow()`;
  - S2 (C13): the id-keyed ones dropped at the fence and taken again after each Live; the rank's blocks (the snapshot, the played set's chunks, the lists, the sparse index) and the walk's folder states after `joinNow()`, at the boot and after each Live, held from the rank to the walk's end, never while the join's `begin()` holds its spacers (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.h:102-105`). It asserts the join's lowest `>= kMargin` with them taken.
- It runs at P = 2,214 and at P = 19,478.
- It holds the mood plan's pass rule (`:2898-2925`): `lowest >= kMargin`, `fence == off.fence`, `givenBack == off.largest`, joined within 122,880 B of the control and at or over 245,764 B where the control is. Opus's 65,536 and 62,464 B blocks must still fit after a FLAC update.
- At bc29b54 the only `GiveBackFn` is Auto DJ's (`src/main.cpp:3901`), and the stats' resident blocks would be held through every build and come out of the update's room (as built since the owner's decision of 2026-11-11, they are given back: below). On the device that room is thin: joined after a FLAC update and the rejoin is 262,132-294,900 B (`docs/MOOD-FILTERS.md:932, 954`), 16,368-49,136 B over the 245,764 B line, less than the typical resident set (11); the mood words' 38,826 B block cost exactly one 32 KB class of joined (`:932`). So the resident blocks must stay out of the largest free block, or rule 3 forces a redesign (`docs/MOOD-FILTERS.md:768-771`).
- **The ladder**, in order, if joined falls by more than 122,880 B, or under 245,764 B where the control is above it (not needed since the give-back, below):
  1. the resident tables in 16 KB chunks (5.6, the design from the start), so they fill the holes the boot leaves: the model shows whether they do;
  2. hash → id for the lists' rows only (696 rows, about 8.4 KB: one chunk), with a walk after any re-rank that brings a new hash, instead of covering RCNT's 4,096 rows as well (saves up to 49 KB);
  3. RCNT capped at 2,048 rows (24.6 KB).
- **As built (S1 C8, MODELLED; re-run after the S1 review), the blocks held through the updates:** `test_the_listening_stats_in_a_model_heap` (`test/test_autodj_join`) carves, at `PlayHistory::begin()`'s place, the glue (`sizeof(statsglue::Glue)`: the Fold is inside it), the Fold's HOUR, DAYS and MARK blocks, then the read's work memory and the load's chunks, the work memory given back, in `Glue::begin()`'s order; after the boot's `joinNow()`, the boot's fold (begun at the scan's first piece: since the S1 review the firmware carves its ranges and indexes there too, not in `setup()`, where they had been carved before the dance's, the UI's and Thumbs' blocks), then the save, or none landed (a refusal: the fold ended alone). It holds them through two update steps of the 19,412-track library, against the run without them. The rule holds at P = 2,214 (1,365 RCNT rows, 1,000 TRKD rows: 80,192 B resident) with a short boot fold (300 slots): joined 249,844 B against the control's 360,436 B (-110,592 B), the fence as off's. It fails with a full never-synced log's fold at P = 2,214 (peak 347,456 B; 129,344 B held after the save; joined 180,212 B, -180,224 B; the fence not off's), at P = 19,478's resident ceiling with a short fold (RCNT at 4,096 rows, TRKD at 4,092: 194,880 B resident; joined 180,212 B, -180,224 B), with both (peak 445,760 B; joined 225,268 B, -135,168 B), and with a full log's fold whose save doesn't land (RCNT held to its cap at the fold's end, 5.6: 145,728 B held at P = 2,214, joined 180,212 B; 194,880 B at P = 19,478, joined 225,268 B). The review's corrections to the carve order (the glue's size, HOUR, DAYS and MARK before the work memory) changed none of the figures. On the device the scan's first pieces can come before the UI's first frame lets the join begin; the model takes the join first (C8 tried the fold before the join: no verdict changed). Carving the load before the library's blocks, or at the boot's end, changes none of these either (tried, not kept). So the chunks don't fill the boot's holes: the resident blocks come out of the update's largest free block (110,592 B of it for 80,192 B resident; 180,224 B for 194,880 B), and ladder step 1 is not enough. The case pinned each shape's figures and then failed, listing the shapes the rule fails for (since the S1 review; it had been reported ignored, which let every Done-when command pass): the next step was the owner's (the ladder's 2-3, which S1 alone can't take further than RCNT's cap, or a redesign: 13). (Since the give-back, the held-through run is kept beside each shape; with the glue's object grown by its ring, the P = 19,478 full-log shapes' held-through joined is 143,348 B, not 225,268 B: a layout's shift, checked by carving the glue at its old 3,160 B. The figures of this paragraph are of 16,384 B chunks; the table below has the held-through run in chunks of whole rows.)
- **The give-back (the owner's decision of 2026-11-11, "Give back in updates"; as built, MODELLED).** During a library update the stats give their resident blocks back, the way Auto DJ already gives its join back, and read `stats.bin` from the card again after it.
  - **When.** At the update's give-back points, after Auto DJ's at each, the order the model runs: the memory check's hook (`LibraryUpdate::setGiveBack()`, set for every boot now: Auto DJ's join, then the stats', PSRAM measured once after both), when the new track table is short of a block; and the fence (`enterFence()`, after Auto DJ's `fence()`, before the old index goes), when the check didn't take them. `UpdateEnv::alsoFreed` counts them (`Glue::resident()`), as it counts the join's. Not while the boot's fold runs; but before it begins (the boot's read loaded, nothing folded) or with a remount's read to make, and the boot's scan waits while an update is asked, so its fold never begins or grows behind the update (as built, the give-back review: 5.6). When the fold holds plays to save first, the update step waits in Asked for a pass the play log's write gate lets through (`Wait::Writes`): the safe point is at least 20-30 s of the heard track left, a track's first seconds and its refill included. It waits there too while a play is queued for its slot (S1's device round 1, `Glue::updateWaits()`), one behind a try the card failed too (its fixes' review; at most 10 s a play the card goes on failing): written first, folded live, and the save holds it, not the ring. Taken again once the update is over (its save done, or it deferred or failed) and Auto DJ's rejoin is done or none is coming (`AutoDj::joinSettled()`), at a pass the write gate lets through, the join not Working, the card not away: a pass that may carve, never `setup()`'s or the card guard's.
  - **What.** RCNT's and TRKD's chunks, the spare, HOUR and DAYS (`statsfold::Fold::giveBack()`). Kept: MARK's block (1,536 B for 64 rows: the card's MARK, so `covered()` answers through the update and the trim and a rotation go by it; RAM's for `markOf()`), carved in `setup()` among the boot's blocks; and the glue's object (`statsglue::Glue`, the Fold inside it: 5,824 B on the 64-bit host since S1's device round 1 grew its ring to 32 slots, 4,544 B with 16, 3,160 B before the give-back: its ring and state, carved in `setup()`; on the device 5,136 B, read with gdb from the core2 ELF after S1's device round 1; 3,840 B at the give-back's review, with 16 slots). Neither is in the update's room.
  - **The save first** (5.5): when the fold holds plays the card's `stats.bin` doesn't, the rotation's path, never a compaction, with a save's guards; the blocks go whatever it comes to. The copy that holds every slot RAM held is noted: the card's copy (nothing new, or the save landed) or the save's own tmp, held (its rename refused: the re-read promotes it); none when it didn't land or the fold had run out of memory.
  - **While given back** (5.5, 5.6): no save (`Refused::GivenBack`; a rotation's too, which then deletes what the card's MARK doesn't cover), Hs refused with that reason, no read or carve; each play written goes into a ring of 32 slots (80 B each; the Tracker's 32, 5.6; 16 until S1's device round 1) in the glue's object, one past it counted.
  - **The re-read** (5.4): `stats.bin` with no waits in the loop, a remount's verdicts; a read the card failed made again at later passes, the boot's waits apart, four in all (the give-back review); off for the session if it doesn't read. Then the ring folded, but a play the re-read's MARK covers. If the read didn't find the copy that held every slot RAM held, or a play went past the ring, the session is behind its log: every later save refused (`Refused::Behind`; the next boot folds what RAM lacks while the log holds it, and a rotation meanwhile loses those in `plays.1.jnl`: 5.5's hole, `update-behind-rotation`). A remount's scan begun again meanwhile waits for it, then reads at its first piece and folds the log again (behind cleared); a boot's scan whose fold hadn't begun waits for it, then folds.
  - **The model.** `StatsRun::giveBack`: at each step's fence, after `join.release()`, a save first where the fold is dirty (plan and saved; or refused, the re-read then loading the boot's file), then `Fold::giveBack()`; after the Live and `joinNow()`, the re-read (`begin()`: HOUR and DAYS; the work memory; the load's chunks and spare; the work memory given back). "Joined" is measured with the stats taken again. "Given back" in the rule is now the next memory check's give-back, the chain the hook makes: the join, then the stats' blocks. Every shape passes the mood plan's rule whole, two update steps of the 19,412-track library, against the control's (no stats) 360,436 B, and since S1's gate after the give-back the device's room too (below, the last column). The figures are of chunks of whole rows (5.6), and at P = 19,478 stats.bin holds RCNT's 4,096 rows and TRKD's 4,096 (the forced compaction's 96 KB; the model had loaded 4,092, what six 16,384 B chunks held):

| Shape | Held after the boot's fold | Given back (first) | Taken again (first) | Joined | Held through instead | Within the device's 16,368 B |

| P = 2,214, 300 slots | 80,224 B | 78,688 B | 96,616 B | 360,436 B (+0) | 249,844 B (-110,592) | yes |
| P = 2,214, a full log, its save landed | 129,400 B | 127,864 B | 129,400 B | 360,436 B (+0) | 180,212 B (-180,224) | yes |
| P = 19,478, 300 slots | 194,968 B | 193,432 B | 178,576 B | 360,436 B (+0) | 249,844 B (-110,592) | yes |
| P = 19,478, a full log, its save landed | 194,968 B | 193,432 B | 178,576 B | 360,436 B (+0) | 180,212 B (-180,224) | yes |
