  The margin holds everywhere (free at least 443,240 B while the join places after the update since the ring's 32 slots, 444,520 B with 16; 116,836 B at P = 19,478 with 16,384 B chunks; no other figure of the table moved with the ring), the fence's frees leave the heap as Auto DJ off's, and given back (the join and the stats) is off's block, 360,436 B; so is the join alone given back, in every shape. With 16,384 B chunks joined fell 16,384 B in two P = 19,478 shapes: the re-read's eleventh chunk, one over 11's budget, came out of the largest free block, the rejoin having taken the holes it would have filled (the join alone given back then left 344,052 B). Tried and not kept then (MEASURED-HOST, the give-back's experiment): taking them again before the rejoin kept the join-alone figure at off's, but joined fell 49,152 B in those shapes instead of 16,384 B.

  **The device's room** (the give-back review, 2026-11-11). The model's joined is about 100 KB over the device's (360,436 B against MOOD-FILTERS Run D's 262,132 B after a FLAC update and the rejoin, moods on), so the rule's 245,764 B clause never binds in it. On the device that room is 16,368 B, less than one re-read chunk: a 16,384 B chunk carves 16,900 B of its block (`ModelHeap::carved(16384)` = 16,896 B, and a 4 B header), so from a raw block of 262,144-270,335 B (what reports 262,132 B) it leaves one that reports 241,652 or 245,748 B, both under the line, unless the raw block was at least 266,756 B. The line is the room for a FLAC and an Opus's 65,540 B block, so the Opus carve after a FLAC update could have failed in the two P = 19,478 shapes that crossed it with 16,384 B chunks. The model also has no `library.idx` save, which the firmware's take-back always comes after (MOOD-FILTERS measured that save moving joined by 114,688 B when it lands after the rejoin). So `test_the_listening_stats_in_a_model_heap` also asks that the take-back cost joined no more than 16,368 B (`statsOn.joined + 16,368 >= on.joined`), and lists and fails the shapes that don't. Two P = 19,478 shapes did, by one chunk, until S1's gate after the give-back found that chunk was one over 11's budget: 16,384 B chunks hold 1,365 RCNT rows and 682 TRKD rows, so RCNT's 4,096-row cap took four chunks, not three (and TRKD's 96 KB seven, not six). Chunks of 16,392 B hold 1,366 and 683, nothing over, and carve the same 16,900 B: the re-read at P = 19,478 is ten chunks, 178,576 B, and every shape's joined is the control's (+0); the case passes whole (MEASURED-HOST). The review's other answer, RCNT's cap at 2,048 rows (ladder step 3: +0 in every shape, the re-read 162,112 B), is not needed. Run L records joined with a P = 19,478 card: the model's joined is about 100 KB over the device's, so the device's own figure is the measure. The last two rows are the update asked before the boot's scan's first piece (the give-back review; before it, the give-back was refused there and the fold began and grew to its peak while the build ran): the loaded blocks given back at the fence, the scan held through the update, the fold after the first take-back, joined measured after the fold's end and save.

- **The rework (2026-11-12; designed and reviewed, R1-R7 built: 5.14 and this section's lead).** The two-step case above can't see what S1's device round 2 found (188,404 B against main's 294,900 B after an Opus update): it runs two updates with no decoder carved between them and measures joined once, so every give-back shape matches the control's there. The multi-update model (5.14: 5 steps, the decoders' long-lived carves as the device asks for them since the rework's review, a `Dc` after each or not, joined per step against main's run in the same history) does see it: S1 as built fails 47 of the wide set's 360 steps with the device's decoder requests (36 with the old shapes), H3's steps 3-4 and the first of round 2's own sequence among them (MEASURED-HOST). It is `test_the_listening_stats_across_updates` since 5.14's R1, which failed on purpose against S1 as built until R6 made `StatsRun` the rework's (the firmware's glue on a card in memory, every block through the model heap): the rework fails none of the 360 with the device's decoders (2 with the old shapes, both the control's), its boot's join main's (5.14's R6 "As built"). The rework's `StatsRun` (one block of at most 3,584 B at `begin()`; the boot's scan's ranges, and a full log's overflow, at the top keeping 64 KB free from the scan's first piece to the fold's end, the update and the join waiting for them; a save's work memory and FatFiles carved and given back between steps, in the fence's pass and at 96 in a fence) fails none of the 360 (the old shapes: 2, both failed by main with its queue block 2,048 B longer). The model doesn't reproduce round 2 on round 2's own sequence, so R1 measures the rework against main's own variation and the device re-run decides. What the model showed that the ladder above didn't: a resident set's cost is its bytes, not its place (step 1 assumed chunks could fill the boot's holes; carved anywhere in setup, a block moves everything after it); from 8 KB a set fails steps main's variation doesn't; and with the device's decoders a block held across the boot's passes costs when the Opus opens under it unless it is small (the ranges' 3,072 B: none; the work memory's 10,896 B: up to 8 of 360; a full log's overflow, about 270 KB: 0-29, none with the Opus opening after it, so a track's start waits while it is held). The give-back, its table and the device's room above are S1 as built's; they went with R5 and R6 (since R6 `test_the_listening_stats_in_a_model_heap` runs the rework's 13 shapes: every joined the control's 360,436 B, within its class, 8,192 B, 5.14's rule).

### 6.8 The rankers and the per-track read (the card worker)

- **One new job**, `ScanScheduler::Job::Stats` (`kJobs` 8 to 9: `lib/core/ScanScheduler.h:101-102`), in two lanes of `ScanScheduler::decide()`. A job `decide()` doesn't name would fall to `priorityOf()`'s default of 0 with no rule (`lib/core/ScanScheduler.cpp:177`). The waveform read's lane is not the rank's: it starts before the update step's save and before any yield check (`:121-125`; `lib/core/ScanScheduler.h:31`, "The audio's yields below don't hold them"), at priority 1, and only background work waits on `updating` (`:134-136`; `.h:38-40`).
  - The **Track** read (one read of at most 1,536 B) runs beside Wave, after it: `In::statsTrack`, priority 1, 0 while a list moves.
  - The **Rank** is background work, after the walk and the compaction and before the scan: `In::statsRank`. It is held by `updating` (so it starts after `library.idx`'s save), by a list moving and by every yield (Input, Ring, Underrun, DecodePass, TrackChange, Seek, Bluetooth). Priority 0 while lit and 1 while dark, as Compact; one step, never cut. A rank reads the whole base (467 KB at 19,478 played, 11) while holding the SPI bus and FatFs's volume lock against the decoder (`src/storage/CardFat.h:33-35`), and the SD driver busy-waits (`src/app/CardWorker.h:24-31`): in the Wave lane it would time-slice with the loop at priority 1 for its whole step.
- **Two kinds of work:**
  - **Rank:** a snapshot of TRKD and RCNT, copied to PSRAM on the loop (merged from the sorted chunks as it is copied: 5.6; its µs on `Hr`), goes to the worker with today and sinceDay. The worker reads the base front to back, merged with the delta, and makes:
    - the lists of 4.3 (each cap plus 16);
    - the played set (6.1);
    - a sparse index of the base, every 64th hash (12 B each: 3,660 B at 19,478).
    The loop takes the result when the step ends, then walks.
  - **Track:** at a track's start (`PlayHistory::file()`, `src/app/PlayHistory.cpp:146-160`), its base row: one read of at most 64 rows (1,536 B) found through the sparse index. No base (baseGen 0): no read. The facts are that row ⊕ its TRKD row.
- **When the lists rank:** after the boot's Ready; after a Live; at a UTC day's change; when a stats page is entered after folds since the last rank. Results show at the next `enter()` of a shelf; the hub's lines refresh in place.
- **Against Auto DJ's join.** "After the boot's Ready; after a Live" are the moments the join begins (`stepJoin` at the first pass after Live or after the first frame: `src/app/AutoDj.cpp:636-642`), and the join's 64 KB margin covers only another task's small blocks, "the card worker's 4-16 KB buffers" (`:122-138`); a block another task takes inside `begin()` makes the placement fall short (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.h:96-105`, `topShort`). The lowest PSRAM free in an update's join is 175,032-180,876 B on the device (`docs/MOOD-FILTERS.md:932`). So:
  - a rank starts only when Auto DJ's join is neither Working nor due: Auto DJ off, or its join Ready since the last Live or boot;
  - Auto DJ's `stepJoin` doesn't begin while a stats rank or walk holds blocks (a `Block` reason beside Build and Reads, `src/app/AutoDj.cpp:638`; `src/app/AutoDj.h:193`);
  - the loop carves every block the rank fills before `worker.start()`: the snapshot, the played set's chunks (at most HEAD.baseRows plus TRKD's rows), the lists and the sparse index. The worker allocates nothing.
- **Between ranks:** a live fold moves its track to Recently played's top and patches its counts in the lists. Nothing is re-sorted on the loop.
- **Compaction** never runs with a Stats step under way: it happens only in a boot's save, before Ready, when no Stats step has begun.

**As built (A, 2026-11-20; C12, C14).** What changed from the list above:
- **`Job::Stats` in its two lanes**, as planned (`lib/core/ScanScheduler`, `kJobs` 9): `In::statsTrack` after the waveform read and before a cover, priority 1, 0 while a list moves; `In::statsRank` after a compaction and before the scan, held by `updating`, a list and every yield, never by the battery floor, priority 0 lit and 1 dark (`rankPriority()`), never cut (`In::runningRank`, `Out::statsRank`).
- **When**: only when asked (`Hr`, `Hl`, `Hw`, `Hp`), one ask at a time; none at the boot's Ready, a Live or a day's change.
- **The rank's input**: the card's own sections and a copy of the records (5.14's "The screens"), not a RAM snapshot of TRKD and RCNT (none since the rework). `statsrank::rank()` merges the base's TRKS, `stats.bin`'s TRKD and the records' rows (`Fold::Cursor`'s, copied on the loop) by hash, and sums RCNT's rows and the records' over the windows; `stats.bin` must be the copy the session stands on (generation, header CRC), the base the one HEAD names (generation, header CRC, rows), every section's CRC and order checked as it streams.
- **No sparse index is kept** (`statsrank::Sparse` exists and is host-tested; the firmware gives `rank()` none): `Hp`'s read (`statsrank::trackRows()`) is a binary search of the base (at most a read a level, `test_stats_rank`) by TRKS's own offset and stride (a newer minor's base: 5.12), with `stats.bin`'s TRKD streamed.
- **A list that needs the clock** (Most played's 7 and 30 days, New finds, Old favourites) with the clock unset is said at once, nothing carved.
- **The blocks**: one, carved on the loop at a pass the write gate lets through (the turn `Hs` and the looks take), before `worker.start()`. At the hand-off (`StatsLists::prepare()`, on the loop, the worker free) the rank's input is taken as the session stands then, the records copied (room for all 40) and `stats.bin` and the base opened (`statsrank::open()`), closed on the loop after the step: the worker allocates nothing. A step the card worker hasn't taken in 30 s (none this boot, or held all that time behind the walk, a compaction and the yields) is given up, its block given back.
- **While the step runs** (handed until taken in) no stats save and no slot is made (`PlayHistory::loop()`: a slot's rotation saves), so nothing replaces `stats.bin` under its reads; while it is only queued, saves and slots go on. The orderly end waits for it (`ListeningStats::ending()`, 3 s at most) before its flush; past that the end goes on, and a rotation's save in the flush may replace `stats.bin` under reads whose result is never taken in.
- **Against Auto DJ's join**, as planned: a rank (and the walk) waits while `AutoDj::joinPending()` (on, the index open, the join Working, or, with the library's index readable, not Ready for it and not blocked by the next build or failed reads); the join doesn't begin while an ask holds its block (`Env::statsHold`, the boot scan's hold or `PlayHistory::listsHold()`).
- **Against the card worker's walk and compactions** (the device pass, a5c2950): a rank's ask doesn't take its block while the card worker has the background work a rank comes after in `ScanScheduler`'s order: the boot's walk not done, a walk armed or under way, a compaction due or running (`StatsLists::workerAhead()`, `CardTasks::loop()`'s word each pass; the line "waits: the card worker's walk or compaction"). Without it, F's `Hw` during the boot took its block when the join ended (10.8 s from the boot) and queued holding it behind the boot's walk (armed after the first frame and held while Bluetooth pages: its 22.6 s of steps done at 51.0 s), and the 30 s queued deadline gave the step up at 40.7 s. `Hp` (the Track lane, before the walk) doesn't wait for it.
- **Between ranks**: nothing is held, so nothing is patched.
- **A library update** waits in Asked while an ask holds its block (`UpdateEnv::statsWait`); an ask waits while an update is asked. `enterFence()` drops a block all the same if one were held (`StatsLists::drop()`: the ask begins again after the Live).

*As decided (2026-11-20, later; S3a, 9.7).* What S3 changes in the rank and its asks. The list above (its "When the lists rank" and "Between ranks" bullets: a rank at the boot, a Live and a day's change, patches between ranks) is history; S2's "As built" stands, but for these changes.
- **The read's order.** `statsrank::rank()` reads RCNT and the records' RCNT rows into the windows first, and finishes them into the Ranker before TRKS ⊕ TRKD ⊕ the records' TRKD stream. The windows' rows are then dead before the first played hash is written, so the u40 played set reuses their room. The lists don't change: each list is its own bounded heap with the hash as the last tie-break (`lib/core/StatsRank.cpp:119-130`). `Outcome::Room` now comes before TRKS is read.
- **DAYS.** The rank also streams `stats.bin`'s DAYS and the records' DAYS rows, copied at the hand-off with the others: the 7-day window's days, and the first and newest dated day (O5's span).
- **Counts.** Every list's offers are counted (`Ranker::total()`) for hub rows 3-4. RCNT's oldest day is reported for `stats.days_n`.
- **The u40 played set** (`statsrank::Played40`: 5 B a hash, the top 40 bits, ascending as streamed, no sort). S2's chunked u64 `Played` stays for `Hw` and `Hl3`, the exact reference.
- **No clock.** No windows' room is carved: `Windows::add()` returns before its room check when undated (`:203-204`).
- **The UI's asks.** Typed asks (a visit, a lean Not played yet), cancel, release, a phase, why, outcome and version; the snapshot carved before the take (*since 2026-11-21*, lent: the Dance tab's block, 9.7); the grades tried in order; the copy-out into the snapshot at `finishRank()`. Only the listener's navigation asks: entering the stats pages, coming back, a wake on one, Try again. Nothing is patched between asks: a page shows its lists as of its ask.
- **One slot, as S2's.** A UI ask meeting a console ask under way waits behind it. A console ask meeting a UI ask is refused "under way" (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:164-166`). S2's "replaced while it hadn't begun" (`:188`) doesn't apply to UI asks.
- **History.** The list's "Rank" work (a RAM snapshot of TRKD and RCNT, a sparse index kept for `Hp`), its "When the lists rank" and its "Between ranks" are the plan before A. S2's "As built" says what replaced them, and these notes what S3 adds.

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21; 9.7's "As built (S3a)"):*
- **The read's order** is DAYS ⊕ the records' DAYS rows first, then RCNT ⊕ the records' RCNT rows into the windows, finished into the Ranker, then the tracks (`lib/core/StatsRank.cpp`, `rank()`). A rank with lists reads both, clock or not: their CRCs are checked as S2's are, RCNT gives its oldest day and DAYS gives O5's span. S2's `Hr`, `Hl` and `Hw` stream DAYS too, so a DAYS section that fails its CRC or its read refuses them (Damaged, Io). The lean ask's played-set-only rank reads neither.
- **The 30 s give-up**, for S3a's asks: the block is given back and the ask held in Asked 30 s from the give-up (`statsvisit::stallHolds()`: the gate's 64, holding nothing), then taken again; no count and no end. S2's asks are still given up. `StatsLists::start()` makes the records' copy and the opens only while the card worker's task is there or its spawn is due (`CardWorker::mayStart()`).
- **The tag scan's memory** (V8; the owner's "Only while held", 2026-11-21): S3a's asks also wait while the card worker's tag scan holds its memory (`statsvisit::kWhyRest`: `CardTasks` hands `restAhead()` each pass, as `workerAhead()`). S2's asks don't: they keep main's behaviour. Not while the battery floor pauses the scan (*as decided, 2026-11-22*, "Don't wait at the floor": `statsvisit::restHolds()`).
- **The lines the "As decided" notes above cite** are de4f52f's. At S3a: the "under way" refusals `src/app/StatsLists.cpp:359-366` (a console ask meeting a UI ask, then S2's), "it hadn't begun" `:408-409`; `Ranker::offer()`'s heap `lib/core/StatsRank.cpp:120-132`, `Windows::add()`'s undated return `:205-206`.

## 7. The UI

### 7.1 Pages and navigation

- **New PageKinds** `StatsHub`, `StatsShelf`, `StatsWeek`, after `AutoDj` (`src/ui/Page.h:20-36`). They are named in `pageKindName()` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:46-64`).
- **`Ui::pageFor()`** (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:268-285`): Hub and Shelf go to `libraryPage_`. So the status band and `shownTracks()` work as on any Library list (`:578-582`, `:1926-1936`). Week goes to a new `statsWeekPage_` (`src/ui/Ui.h:590-595`).
- **Depths:** the root (Artists) is 1, the hub 2, a shelf or Week 3 (with the crumb: `src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:387`), an album from Not played yet 4 (`kMaxDepth` 8: `lib/core/NavModel.h:28`). Back is "‹" (x 0-55), the crumb (x 240-319), or the Library tab.
- **PageRef.id** is `shelf * 4 + window`: shelves 0 Most played, 1 New finds, 2 Old favourites, 3 Not played yet, 4 Recently played; windows 0 seven days, 1 thirty, 2 All time. The window last used is kept per shelf for the session. `segment()` reads `id_ <= 2` (`src/ui/Pages.h:468`), so shelf ids 0-2 look like segments: keep every use of it behind `root()`.
- **From Now Playing:** `showLibrary(Artists, {StatsHub, StatsShelf(scrollPx = kShowPlaying)}, 2)` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:639-656`). `showLibrary()` puts the root back on Artists (`:644-649`), as Go to artist does.
- *As decided (2026-11-20, later; calls 1-2):*
  - **No page object of its own for Week.** `pageFor()` gives all three kinds to `libraryPage_`, and Week is drawn through a body hook (7.2 item 6, 7.7). So `ui::Ui` gains no member, and its carve stays main's: `sizeof(ui::Ui)` in its TLSF class, read with gdb on both ELFs, as 9.6 read Auto DJ's object.
  - **Most played's window** is kept for the visit, in a byte of the snapshot's header, written by `statsSetWindow()` (7.11) at a tray tap; the shelf's `PageRef.id` carries it too. A new visit opens on All time (O1). Across the dark, the nav stack's `PageRef.id` keeps the window shown.
  - **On the stats pages** means: the Library tab is shown, its stack holds a StatsHub, StatsShelf or StatsWeek ref (an album, artist page, bar or sheet above one counts), and the screen is lit. `statsview::onStatsPages()` is pure and host-tested. The snapshot is held exactly while this is true (9.7, "The snapshot's life"). *As decided (2026-11-21, "Borrow Dance's block"):* the snapshot is the Dance tab's block, lent, so the Dance tab coming up releases it too (S3a's `dancelend::reclaim()`). A stats page and the Dance tab never show at once, so S3b's `Ui` needs no edge of its own for it: its tab change releases the snapshot anyway, and whichever release comes first, the other finds nothing (one `[listen]` line). On the way back, `Ui::showTab()` calls the Dance page's `leave()` (`DanceMode::setActive(false)`, `src/ui/DancePage.cpp:49`) before the Library page's `enter()` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:633`), and the ask is taken passes later, with the tab down.

### 7.2 What ListLayout and ListView need

The list's items are 42 px (`lib/core/ListLayout.h:24`), with at most one item 0 (`:34-38`). Most played needs the tray and the top bar both.

1. **`Kind::TopRow`, a lead item ahead of the top bar.** `ListLayout::set(rows, topBar, topRow)` with a lead count of 0-2, used by `itemCount()` (`lib/core/ListLayout.h:38`), `item()` and `itemOfRow()` (`lib/core/ListLayout.cpp:6-41`). `ListView` handles it like `topRow(it)` today (`src/ui/ListView.h:424-427`): `renderItem` (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:423-473`), `tapItem` (`:1225-1266`), the Down (`:1307-1345`). Host tests beside `test/test_ui_nav/test_ui_nav.cpp:183`.
2. **Inert rows** for the disabled hub rows. `pressable()` false ignores a tap (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:1251`), and a Tap acts on `pressedItem_` (`:1373-1379`), which an unpressed row never sets. New `Source::inert(row)` and `onInertTap(row)`: the item is remembered at the Down without the press look; a Tap on it, the list still, calls `onInertTap()` with no tick.
3. **`refreshTopRow()`** beside `refreshRow()`, which takes data rows only (`:304-311`): the entry row's and the tray's redraw in place.
4. **`recountStaged()`** beside `reload()`, which always redraws the whole band (`:288-297`: 35-50 ms idle, 80-100 ms with an MP3: `src/ui/ListView.h:60-62`). It sets the layout, keeps the lead items, and pushes one stale on-screen item a pass. An edge change (312 to 320 px) falls back to `reload()`.
5. **The empty line under the lead items**, at `kTop + lead * 42` (`drawEmpty()`, `src/ui/ListView.cpp:584-601`). Today it draws only with no items or under a row-like item 0 (`:484`).

*As decided (2026-11-20, later):*
- **Item 4** stages an edge change too, 312 to 320 px wide (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:204-213`), instead of falling back to `reload()`. A shelf's rows arriving from the ask (0 to N, a pass or two after its push) are staged one a pass at a still pass: no bar or sheet open, the list not moving (OutputPage's `still`, `src/ui/OutputPage.cpp:281`). No whole-band `reload()` comes from an arrival.
- **The shelf's place** (the scroll, `kShowPlaying`) is kept in its `PageRef` while it has 0 rows: `ListView` doesn't `saveRef()` while its source says its rows are pending (a new `Source::pending()`, no bytes). Otherwise `:820` would write the 0-row scroll over it (`:225-231`). When the rows land the scroll is put back, clamped to the new rows.
- **An open bar across a new ask.** Inside a visit no row moves, so nothing needs putting back. A new ask (coming back from another tab, a wake, Try again) can move rows: a play counted meanwhile gives Recently played a new row 0, and reorders Most played. So at a new ask's landing the bar isn't reopened (`expanded` −1), whatever the ref says, and the scroll is kept. A bar reopened by its index could act on a track the listener didn't pick (`LibraryPage::onAction()` acts on `rowAt(row)` at the tap: `src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:926-929`). `listlayout::arrival(ref, rows, sameVisit, heightPx)`, pure in `lib/core/ListLayout`, gives the place (`test_ui_nav`).
- **A free while a stats page is attached** (the dark, the card away, the stats off, a remount's key that no longer matches): in the same call the page is attached again with 0 rows, as `leave()` then `enter()` would (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:313-316`, `:281`). An open bar folds into its ref, a sheet closes (`closeModal()`, `src/ui/Ui.cpp:788`; `setDark(true)` itself closes none: `:2348-2369`). Without it the attached list would keep N cached rows over freed memory (`ListView::attach()` caches the count: `src/ui/ListView.cpp:225`), and a wake, which redraws the attached page straight away (`Ui::setDark(false)` → `redrawAll()` → `repaint()`, `src/ui/Ui.cpp:2378`, `:2383`, `:2402`; no `enter()`, no `attach()`; `LibraryPage` has no `screenOff()`), would draw them. A wake or a returning card then takes the 0-to-N path above.
- **New item 6, a body hook** (`Source::drawBody(canvas, y0, h)`), drawn where the empty state draws (`drawEmpty()`) when a page with no items asks for it. Week uses it (7.7).
- **No item for patches:** the rows never change under an open page (no patches).
- **Where S3b's UI bytes live** (call 2's reading, section 1): C16's staging cursor (the next stale item, 2 B) and the inert row remembered at the Down (2 B) are `ListView`'s; the hub's arrival cursor (1 B) and the last minute drawn (2 B) are `LibraryPage`'s. Both are members of `ui::Ui` (`src/ui/Ui.h:528`, `:591`), about 8 B with padding, so S3b's gate reads `sizeof(ui::Ui)` against main's class (gdb). Nothing else of the UI's grows: the phase, the version and the news byte are `StatsLists`' `.bss`; the window and the lists are the snapshot's.

### 7.3 The way in: Library > Artists, item 0 (frame Entry)

`topBar()` and `topBarIsRow()` become true on the Artists root while the row is due (today only Albums has a top row: `src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:584-591`); `drawTopRow()` and `onTopRowTap()` (`:1101-1152`) dispatch by segment. The geometry is FilterGrid's first row (`lib/core/FilterGrid.h:193-216`):

| Part | Geometry |
|---|---|
| Row | Item 0, 42 tall, scrolls with the list; 284 wide beside the rail; ROW_SEL when pressed; divider from x 8 |
| Bars (the icon) | 7 bars 4 px wide on a 5 px step, x 9-43, standing on y 32; 24 px is the top day; past days DIM, today violet (`accent::Library`); no plate |
| Title | "Listening stats", Body TXT, x 54, centred on y 13; room 204 |
| Line | Small DIM, y 31, room 204: `stats.entry_sub`, else `stats.entry_sub_short` (de needs it), else cut |
| Chevron | FAINT, at right − 14 |

- **Tap:** ROW_SEL at the Down; at the lift a 33 ms tick, then the hub.
- **No hold:** a long press ends as a tap (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:1380-1391`).
- **Hits:** the row is x 0-279; the rail keeps x 280 on (`:1308`).
- *As decided (2026-11-20, later; call 2, "Lifetime line, no memory"):*
  - **The line** is the lifetime one: `stats.since` ("74 h 10 min since 1 Sep": `format.hours_minutes` of HEAD's listenedMs, and `format.day_month` of `Fold::sinceDayNow()`). It is RAM, drawn at `enter()`. In a language where it is wider than 204 px, the time alone is drawn; with sinceDay unset, the time alone as well. `stats.entry_sub` and `stats.entry_sub_short` are dropped (8.2).
  - **The bars** are a fixed icon: seven bars at the fixed heights `statsview::kEntryBars`, coloured as the frame (past DIM, the last violet). They are not data.
  - **Asks:** none from the entry row.
  - **Its other states** (Folding, Empty, absent) are 4.4's, from RAM, each with the fixed icon. At the boot fold's end (Folding to the lifetime line) the row is redrawn in place (`refreshTopRow`) if Artists shows (7.10). It isn't redrawn at a later fold: the line is as of `enter()`.

### 7.4 The hub (frame Main)

- **Header**, depth 2: "Listening stats" Bold. The sub (Small DIM), "74 h 10 min since 1 Sep" (155 px), doesn't fit the 139 px beside the title and goes under it (`subBelow`: title y 10, sub y 26).
- **Rows:** six of 42 px, 312 wide; a thin scrollbar at x 314 (thumb 107 px).
- **The row recipe:** icon 1-bit SOFT, centred at (26, 21), drawn there directly (`ListView::icon` centres at x+22); title Body TXT x 54, y 13; line Small DIM y 31, room 232; chevron FAINT at x 295.

| Row | Icon (new unless said) | Line |
|---|---|---|
| Last 7 days | The 7 bars | `stats.time_plays`: "7 h 37 min · 118 plays" |
| Most played | Trophy 18 x 14 | The All time top three, " · " between, cut with "…" |
| New finds | Spark 18 x 18 | `stats.new_finds_sub` (de: the short form) |
| Old favourites | Heart 18 x 15 | `stats.old_favourites_sub` |
| Not played yet | Ring with a play mark 18 x 18 | `stats.since`: "612 albums since 1 Sep" |
| Recently played | Clock 18 x 18 | "Slow Lantern · 25 min ago" |

- **Disabled row:** icon FAINT, title and reason DIM, no chevron, no press look (the house never uses FAINT for text). A tap: the inert double buzz and the amber toast (4.5).
- **The status row** (no dates), in row 1's place: the clock icon in AMBER; line 1 Small AMBER "No dates yet: the clock isn't set" (206 of 250); line 2 Small DIM "Plug in to the MP3 Player tab" (186 of 250). No press, no tick, no chevron (the precedent: `src/ui/Pages.h:745-748`).
- **Taps:** a row ticks and pushes. Rows are hit across the whole width. No holds: `holds()` excludes the new kinds (today it is true for all but None and Disc: `src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:951-954`).
- **Scrolling:** a swipe up from the strip scrolls the list, as on any list (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:1295-1305`). No haptics.
- *As decided (2026-11-20, later; 4.4's phases):*
  - **At once:** the header and its sub (lifetime, RAM), the rows' titles and icons (row 1's is the entry row's fixed bars, `kEntryBars`, not the week), and the disabled and status rows (from the counting days and the clock, RAM).
  - **Pending:** each data line reads the phase's line, "Counting plays…" (Working) or "Waiting for the card…" (Waiting). With No room, Unread or Capped the data lines are empty and a Try again row follows row 6: its title `empty.try_again` (Body, the row recipe), its line the reason (`stats.no_room`, `stats.unread` or `stats.waiting`, Small DIM, room 232), pressable, with the row's tick. The reason has a row of its own, so it never meets the status row, which holds row 1's place with no dates.
  - **When the ask lands,** the lines are redrawn in place one a pass (`refreshRow`, about 11 ms each).
  - **Row 5** shows `stats.since` with the count only after a Full or lean Not played yet ask; otherwise `stats.since_only` ("since 1 Sep").
  - **Row 6** is the snapshot's Recently played row 0.
  - **Rows 1-6** are disabled or opened by `notPlayedOpen()` and 4.4.
  - **A disabled row's tap,** a shelf's push and Back within the visit read RAM.

### 7.5 The empty state (frame NoPlays)

The hub with nothing played has 0 rows and no top bar, so it shows the firmware's empty state (`src/ui/EmptyState.cpp:26-80`):
- the header: "Listening stats" alone;
- a 48 px CARD disc at y 103 holding the chart icon (new, 18 x 18) in SOFT;
- "No plays yet" in Title (room 304);
- "Plays count from 30 s or half a track" and "Your stats stay on this card", Small DIM;
- one violet "Shuffle all" with `icons::kShuffle`, x 70-249, y 198-231. It calls `ui_.shuffleAll()` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:433`), with its tick.

`onEmptyAction()` (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:673-685`) branches on `kind_`: today's handler rescans the library or retries the card.

*As decided (2026-11-20, later):* the empty state is decided from RAM (HEAD's plays 0, none this session), so a hub in it asks nothing and holds no snapshot.

### 7.6 The shelves (frames MostPlayed, NewFinds, EarlyDays, NoClock)

**Header**, depth 3: the title Bold in x 28-207; the window's sub under it (`subBelow`), so it stays after the tray scrolls away; the crumb (99 x 26 at x 215).

| Shelf | Sub | Items before the rows | Rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| Most played | "last 7 days", "last 30 days", "since 1 Sep" | The tray (when offered), then the top bar (when there are rows) | Ranked: "Lumen Ferry · 12 plays" |
| New finds | "first heard in 30 days" | The top bar | Ranked: "Sefa Ilmar · found by Auto DJ · 9 plays" ("found by shuffle"; nothing when the listener picked it) |
| Old favourites | "not in 30 days" | The top bar | Ranked: "Halvard Moss · 9 plays · last 14 Sep" |
| Not played yet | "since 1 Sep" | None | Album rows with covers (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:707-719`), A-Z, the rail over 30 rows. A tap opens the album; a hold, its sheet |
| Recently played | None | The top bar | Section rows ("Today", "Yesterday", "Sun 8 Nov" in `format.weekday_date`; "This session", "Earlier"), then rows "Lumen Ferry · 25 min ago" |

**The tray** (Most played; the root's segmented look, `src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:334-363`):
- a CARD tray at (6, 7), 300 x 28, r10;
- three 96 px cells at x 8, 108, 208, or two of 148 px at x 8, 160;
- labels Bold centred on y 21 ("7 days", "30 days", "All time"), Small when Bold doesn't fit 92 (144 for two);
- the picked cell: a BTN_HI plate 96 x 24 r8 at y 9, its label TXT; the others DIM;
- a pressed cell: the tray's BG refilled and that cell alone lit, as the Filters row does its cross (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:1120-1124`). Not `renderItem`'s whole-width ROW_SEL (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:430-433`).

| Counting days | Cells |
|---|---|
| Under 7, or no dates | No tray |
| 7-29 | "7 days", "All time" |
| 30 on | "7 days", "30 days", "All time" |

- A window kept for the shelf that isn't offered falls back to All time.
- RCNT capped (5.2) shortens the 30-day window: its cell then reads `stats.days_n` of the days it holds ("16 days").

**Ranked rows** (the album page's track recipe):
- rank "01", Small DIM, right-aligned at x 30, y 21 (`ListView::number()`, `src/ui/ListView.cpp:1405-1410`); Recently played leaves the column empty;
- title Body, x 40, y 13;
- line Small DIM, y 31, room 264: the count's part measured first, the artist cut to what is left, the count never cut;
- the playing row: ROW_SEL, the still violet EQ glyph in the rank's place (`:1412-1417`), the title Bold violet. `playing()` (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:252-266`) learns the new kind, so `kShowPlaying` finds the row.

**Section rows** are drawn as Disc rows (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:743-751`), Bold violet at x 12, y 25. They aren't pressable and have no hold.

**Behaviour:**
- A tap on a ranked row opens its inline bar (Play, Play next, + Queue). Play plays the shelf from that track.
- A hold (500 ms) gives a double tick and the track's sheet, "Auto DJ from here" first when it applies (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:960-994`).
- The top bar acts on the whole list (`container()`, `pageTracks()`: `:160-217`), named by the shelf's title.
- A shelf with 0 rows (counting, or a window with nothing) has no top bar. Its one line draws under the tray: "Counting plays…" or "Nothing in this period.". Without the 7.2 change the line would never draw (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:484`).
- The rows are the page's snapshot, copied into a PSRAM buffer at `enter()` (as FilteredAlbums is: `lib/core/FilteredAlbums.h:11-25`), kept until the next `enter()`. A re-rank never moves rows under an open bar or a held sheet (`held_.index`: `src/ui/Pages.h:511-512`). A fold patches counts in place.
- The snapshot holds ids of the current index: dropped wherever `dropFilteredAlbums()` is called (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:731-767`).
- *As decided (2026-11-20, later; call 1). The three bullets above are rewritten:*
  - **Where the rows come from.** They are the visit's snapshot (9.7), made once by the hub's ask, not a copy at each `enter()`. A shelf pushed inside the visit draws with its rows (RAM).
  - **Before the ask lands,** a shelf has 0 rows and the phase's line under its tray: "Counting plays…" or "Waiting for the card…". With No room, Unread or Capped it has one row, Try again, drawn as the hub's (7.4: its title `empty.try_again`, its line the reason), first under the tray. It is a row, not the empty state's button: under a lead item the empty line is drawn alone and its buttons can't be pressed (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:584-587`; the buttons only with no rows and no items, `:1271`). The rows then arrive staged (7.2).
  - **Nothing patches the rows.** A fold changes no row; a re-rank happens only at the next visit. Within a visit a row never moves under an open bar or a held sheet, since none moves at all. Across visits rows can move, so a new ask's landing doesn't reopen a bar (7.2).
  - **How a shelf acts.** Its rows are u16 ids in the snapshot, between their facts, and `LibraryPage::act()` takes a `LibraryIndex::Span` of u32 ids (`lib/core/LibraryIndex.h:224-228`; `src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:790-820`). So `QueueModel`'s `replace()`, `insertNext()` and `append()` and `PlaybackController`'s `playNow()`, `playNext()` and `addToQueue()` gain an id-view form (`IdView`: the first id's address, the count, the stride and the width, u16 or u32), which the span forms call. A shelf's Play (from the tapped track: its place in the shelf, `held_.index` the same), Play next, + Queue, its top bar and its sheet pass a view over the shelf's rows in the snapshot. Nothing is copied or carved: a transient u32 array would be held across `playNow()`'s decoder open (`startCurrent()`), as a track's 4 KB buffer in a hold was. Not played yet's album rows act through `container()` as Albums' do. The queue copies the ids (`lib/core/PlaybackController.cpp:696-728`), so the snapshot may be freed after. Cost: as an album's act.
  - **The snapshot** is freed beside `dropFilteredAlbums()` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:735`, `:752`, `:762`: Live, the fence, `browse`), and at the other free points of 9.7. *Since V8 (2026-11-21):* released, not freed (the block is the Dance tab's), and the Dance tab coming up is one of the release points. A shelf's id view over the snapshot stays good through its act: only `DanceMode::setActive(true)` takes the block back, and no act brings the Dance tab up.
  - **Not played yet** reads its albums through the snapshot's bits and A-Z directory (`statsvisit::albumAt()`), not a u32 list (6.5). An album opened from it, and Back, ask nothing.
  - **When Full was refused** (the visit took Lists), Not played yet opens on its No room state: "Not enough memory right now" with Try again, which makes the lean Not played yet ask (9.7). Its bits go into the same snapshot at its landing. Opening the shelf asks nothing by itself: the listener's Try again does, one of the ask points (9.7).
  - **Recently played's "This session"** (no clock): rows whose lastOrd is the ask's `Fold::liveRound()`, recorded by the copy-out.

**Disabled and early states** follow 4.4: the reasons are `stats.needs_dates` and `stats.warming` ("Opens 6 Dec, after a month of plays"; short form "Opens on 6 Dec").

### 7.7 Last 7 days (frame Week), a page of its own

`StatsWeekPage : Page`, modelled on DancePage (`src/ui/Pages.h:671-699`): `scrolls()` false, `hasHeader()` true. Its header copies `LibraryPage::onEvent()`'s header half (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:497-539`) and `Header::hit()` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:68-77`). Not a LibraryPage, so the status band never shows on it.

| y | Region |
|---|---|
| 36-71 | Header, depth 3: "Last 7 days" Bold; "4–10 Nov (UTC)" Small DIM beside it at x 140 when it fits 207, else under; the crumb |
| 72-121 | A: "Listening time" Small DIM at x 16, y 84; the stat value "7 h 37 min" on baseline y 106 (digits Title, units Small SOFT); at the right, "Plays" Small DIM over "118" Title, right-aligned at x 304 |
| 122-189 | B: 7 columns on a 41 px pitch from x 16; bars 24 wide on a 1 px DIV baseline at y 170; 44 px is the top day; past DIM, today violet; no bar for a day before counting; day names (3 letters, Small) centred on y 180, today's TXT |
| 190-239 | C: a 1 px DIV rule at y 193, x 16-304; line 1 Small SOFT at y 207, the big day ("Saturday was your big day: 2 h 1 min"); line 2 Small DIM at y 225, Auto DJ's rate, only when it started 5 or more tracks |

- **No targets** on the page body; back is "‹" or the crumb. The 41 px columns are narrower than the panel's skew past x 240.
- **Drawing:**
  - Three 56-row strips (`src/ui/Gfx.h:35`). The strip isn't kept between uses (`:43-44`), so the whole picture is drawn into each strip at its offset, as `drawEmptyState` does (`src/ui/EmptyState.cpp:68-80`).
  - "Listening time" (rows 76-92) and the value (85-111) share rows, and `Fonts::draw` fills its whole line (`src/ui/Fonts.h:77-84`). Both are drawn glyphs only (bg == fg, `src/ui/Fonts.cpp:585-589`) over a filled strip, the TitleLayout rule (`lib/core/TitleLayout.h:21-24`).
  - **The stat value:** `drawStat(canvas, x, baseline, align, pattern, args)` walks `tr(S::format_hours_minutes)`: placeholders in Title at baseline − 8, literal runs in Small at baseline − 5 (Polish "{0} godz. {1} min", `i18n/pl.json:946`). Its width function is in lib/core, so `test_ui_library` checks it in every language.
- Never reached without dates: the hub's row 1 is then the status row.
- *As decided (2026-11-20, later; calls 1-2):*
  - **Its figures** (A's time and plays, B's seven columns, C's big day and Auto DJ's rate) come from the snapshot's 7 days, as of the hub's ask. It is usually reached after the hub's ask has landed, but not always: a tap on row 1 before the landing, a wake on Week, or a return from another tab asks again.
  - **Its phases:** Working and Waiting draw A's value as the phase's line ("Counting plays…", "Waiting for the card…") with B and C empty, through the body hook. No room, Unread and Capped draw the firmware's empty state instead (`drawEmptyState()`, `src/ui/EmptyState.cpp`): the disc with the chart icon, no title, the reason as its Small line (room 304) and one button, `empty.try_again`, in its existing Bold 168 room. Week has no rows and no items, so that button can be pressed (`src/ui/ListView.cpp:1271`).
  - **No fold redraw:** a play counted while Week shows doesn't move it. 7.10's "fold while Week shows" row goes.
  - **A UTC day's change** while it shows keeps the ask's week. The next visit has the new one.
  - **It is not a `Page` of its own** (7.1): `pageFor(StatsWeek)` is `libraryPage_` with 0 items and no top bar. The picture is drawn by the body hook (7.2 item 6) into each strip at its offset, as this section's drawing rules say. `LibraryPage` leaves the status band off for this kind and gives it no list targets.
  - **As before:** its header and crumb are `LibraryPage`'s, at depth 3. The layout above is unchanged.

### 7.8 Now Playing's navigation sheet (frame NowPlayingSheet)

- `openNavMenu()` (`src/ui/NowPlayingPage.cpp:1039-1097`) opens 4 rows when `statsTrack()` has the facts and a target (the table below), else today's 3. The new row goes first, as "Auto DJ from here" did, so Go to artist, album and folder keep their places.
- With 4 rows the sheet's top is 240 − (36 + 160 + 4) = 40 (`lib/core/SheetLayout.h:25-39`). The new row is at screen y 76-115.
- Its label: "Go to Most played" (Body, 144 px) or "Go to Recently played".
- Its detail: "9 plays · last Sun · 1 skip" (Small DIM, 162 px). It doesn't fit the 128 px beside the label, so it goes under it (label y 13, detail y 31), the sheet's own rule (`src/ui/Overlays.cpp:416-420`).
- Buffers: a label 64 B, a detail 96 B (`src/ui/Overlays.h:241-242`). `@meta` sets `bytes` so `test_every_text_fits_its_buffers` catches a long translation.
- `onSheet()` (`src/ui/NowPlayingPage.cpp:1310-1320`) shifts choices by one when the row is up. Choice 0 calls `goToStats()`: `goToLibrary()`'s checks (`:1130-1147`), then the shelf at `kShowPlaying` (All time for Most played: its count is all-time).

| The track | Row 1 |
|---|---|
| Its row not read yet, or the lists not ranked since the boot (as decided, 2026-11-20, later: no snapshot is held while Now Playing shows, call 1; S4 decides how its row learns the top 50 and Recently played's 200, an ask of its own when the sheet opens being the plain way, and puts it to the owner) | None (3 rows) |
| No counted play before this one | None. No inert row |
| In the All time top 50 | "Go to Most played" and its facts |
| Else in Recently played's 200 | "Go to Recently played" and its facts |
| Else | None |

- The detail: the plays (`stats.plays_n`); "last Sun" within 6 days, "today" and "yesterday", else "last 24 Oct" (UTC); the skips (`stats.skips_n`), left out at 0. No "last" part with no dated play.
- `kShowPlaying` opens at the track playing now, not at `navTrack_` (`src/ui/LibraryPage.cpp:298-310`). A track that changed while the sheet was up opens the shelf at the new one, or at its top.
- Also: the ✕ hit area runs from x 250; rows ignore taps in the first 300 ms; a tab tap closes the sheet as a cancel.

### 7.9 Hits and the touch skew

- x reads right on some panels: +20 px at x 190, +35-45 from 240 (`lib/core/FilterGrid.h:49-56`).
- Rows are hit across their whole width. Every new small target that reaches the right edge starts at least 40 px in.
- **The tray** splits at each gap's middle plus `filtergrid::skew()` (`lib/core/FilterGrid.h:105`: 0 under x 150, 6 under 190, else 13):
  - three cells: 106 + 0 = 106, and 206 + 13 = 219, so x 0-105, 106-218, 219-319;
  - two cells: 158 + 6 = 164;
  - a reading clamped at the edge is the last cell.
  The spec's 114 / 220 were drawn by eye. The host test requires each cell's centre to hit its cell on the lab panel and a true one, by at least 16 px, as the mood grid's.
- The top bar's thirds, the crumb (x 240 on), the sheet's ✕ (x 250 on) and the rail (x 280 on) are as today.

### 7.10 Redraw rules (no tap over 35 ms)

| Event | In its pass | Then |
|---|---|---|
| Push the hub or a shelf | The band: 35-50 ms idle, 80-100 ms with an MP3 (any Library push today) | — |
| Tap a tray cell | The tray via `refreshTopRow` (about 3.4 ms to render, 6 to push) and the header (about 5 ms) | `recountStaged`: one row a pass (about 11 ms each); at most 4 stale, since the tray is on screen. The scrollbar at rest |
| Push Week | The header and three 320 x 56 strips, about 36 ms | — |
| A fold while Week shows | Today's bar column and the two numbers through `gfx::pushRect` (`src/ui/Gfx.h:71`), 1-2 ms | Block C (one 320 x 18 strip, about 3 ms) only when its text changed |
| A fold while a list shows | That track's row, if on screen (about 11 ms) | — |
| The lists ranked or named | Rows whose text hash changed, on screen, one a pass | — |
| A new minute | Lines whose text changed ("25 min ago"), one a pass | — |
| A new UTC day | Week whole; the hub and entry row, staged | A re-rank |
| The NP sheet opens | 4 rows: 34-39 ms, as the playback sheet | — |

No animation. Covers come from the thumb cache. Nothing reads the card in `enter()` or on a tap.

*As decided (2026-11-20, later).* Rows changed and added:

| Event | In its pass | Then |
|---|---|---|
| Enter the stats pages (the hub's push, a tab back onto one) | The push as above. `Ui`'s held condition turns true at the navigation, and its edge queues the visit's ask (a flag, µs). The ask's turn waits for the Rank lane's waits to clear, 500 ms of input quiet among them, so a tap's pass never carves, reads `stats.bin`'s directory or opens files | The ask runs on the card worker and the loop (9.7) |
| A wake on a stats page | `redrawAll()` draws the page as the free left it: 0 rows and the phase's line (7.2). The held condition's edge at the dark's change queues the ask; no `enter()` runs | As above |
| The ask lands (counts and names together, after the walk) | `finishRank()` (the wanted set's seal, then the copy-out) ran in an earlier pass; this pass takes in the result and `resolve()`. Each piece under 35 ms by run 3a-2's rule (the seal a heap sort: 9.7, V2) | Hub lines `refreshRow` one a pass (about 11 ms each). A shelf at 0 rows: the rows staged one a pass at a still pass (7.2), never a whole-band `reload()` |
| The walk while lit | Slices of 100 tracks, up to 4 ms a pass, none in a pass that handled an input event (dark: S2's slices of 250 tracks, up to 8 ms a pass, `src/app/StatsLists.cpp:32`; the lit cap's form walks lit that way too: 9.7) | — |
| The boot fold's end while Artists shows | The entry row's line, Folding's "Counting plays…" to the lifetime line, by `refreshTopRow` (about 11 ms) | — |
| A fold while a page shows | Nothing (no patches) | The next visit's ask has it |
| A new UTC day | Nothing re-ranked. The hub's disabled rows and the tray follow the counting days at the next `enter()` (RAM) | The next visit |
| A new minute | Recently played's and row 6's "25 min ago", from the snapshot's time and now, as above | — |
| A shelf's act (Play, Play next, + Queue, the top bar, the sheet) | As an album's: the queue reads the ids through the id view (7.6), no copy | — |

These rows go: "A fold while Week shows", "A fold while a list shows", "The lists ranked or named" (became the arrival row) and "A new UTC day ... a re-rank". The turn's `lookFile()`, the hand-off's two opens, `finishRank()` and `resolve()` run on the loop task outside `Ui::loop`, so `[ui] slow pass` (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:1535`) doesn't see them, and a touch that lands while one runs waits for it. Run 3a-2 times each and holds each under 35 ms.

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):* the landing runs in the walk's last pass: its last slice, then `resolve()` and `notPlayed()`, and the block is given back. The landing's line gives each loop piece in µs: the turn (`stats.bin`'s directory among it, the turn's own lines left out), the hand-off (the records' copy, the opens), `finishRank()` (the seal, `write()`), `resolve()` and `notPlayed()`. A take that ends without landing gives its pieces so far ("the take's figures (not landed)").

### 7.11 Where the code goes

- **`lib/core/StatsView.{h,cpp}`** (pure, host-tested in `test_ui_stats`): the phase from its inputs; hub row kinds by state; which tray cells; `trayCellAt()`; Recently played's sections (as `librarytext::Discs`, `lib/core/LibraryText.h:120-140`); bar heights; the NP row's choice; `notPlayedOpen(countingDays, dated)` (O5; false while not dated).
- **`lib/core/StatsText.{h,cpp}`** (pure, host-tested): each line's form and its short forms; "artist cut, count kept"; relative times; day labels with `tzOffsetMin`; the stat value's segments and width.
- **`src/ui/LibraryStats.cpp`**: the stats half of `LibraryPage` (`LibraryPage.cpp` is 1,242 lines). New RowKinds `StatsShelf`, `StatsDisabled`, `StatsStatus`, `Ranked`, `Section` (`src/ui/Pages.h:457`); the branches of `rows`, `rowAt`, `topBar`, `topRow`, `drawRow`, `holds`, `pressable`, `onTap`, `header`, `emptyText`, `emptyState`, `onEmptyAction`, `update`, `enter`, `leave`, `describe`.
- **`src/ui/StatsWeekPage.cpp`**, and `drawStat` in `src/ui/Fonts.*`.
- **`src/ui/NowPlayingPage.cpp`**: `openNavMenu`, `onSheet`, `goToStats`; members beside `navTrack_` (`src/ui/Pages.h:327-330`): `navStats_`, `navShelf_`, `navStatsDetail_[96]`.
- **`src/ui/UiHost.h`:** `AppState` gains a `statsview::State stats` (phase, dated, countingDays, sinceDay, version, totals, the 7 days), as `autodjview::State autoDj` (`:145`). Host calls, RAM only: `statsShelf(shelf, window, out)`, `statsHub(out)`, `statsWeek(out)`, `statsTrack(out)`.
- **6 icons** (trophy, spark, heart, clock, chart, ring-play) made by `tools/ui_icons.py` into `src/ui/IconData.cpp`, declared in `src/ui/Icons.h:18-52`.
- *As decided (2026-11-20, later; calls 1-2, the UI's object not grown):*
  - **`lib/core/StatsVisit.{h,cpp}`** (new, S3a): the snapshot's layout, the copy-out and resolve, the grades' layouts, and `phaseOf()`, the phase from the ask's why, outcome and wait (9.7).
  - **`lib/core/ListLayout`**: `listlayout::arrival()`, the place when a shelf's rows land (7.2).
  - **`lib/core/QueueModel` and `PlaybackController`**: the id-view forms of their three adds (7.6), host-tested in `test_queue` beside the span forms.
  - **`statsview`** gains:
    - the phases' lines (Working, Waiting, NoRoom, Unread, Capped, and the state's own);
    - `onStatsPages(stack, tab, dark)`;
    - `notPlayedOpen(countingDays, dated, datedSpanDays)`, which replaces the two-input form (O5);
    - `kEntryBars`, the entry row's fixed bars;
    - Week's figures from the snapshot's 7 days, not from HOUR.
  - **`StatsWeekPage.cpp`** isn't made. Week is `LibraryStats.cpp`'s body hook (7.7).
  - **`UiHost`** gains no `AppState` field. Its calls are RAM, µs:
    - `statsAsk(kind)`, `statsCancel()`, `statsRelease()`;
    - `statsPhase(out)` (phase, why, outcome, version);
    - `statsNews()` (true once each time the version moves: one byte of `StatsLists`' `.bss`, not the UI's);
    - `statsSnapshot()` (the held snapshot once the visit's ask has landed, its lists resolved; else null, so a block being written is never read. A later lean ask doesn't hide it: its bits are written only at its own landing);
    - `statsSetWindow(window)` (Most played's window into the held snapshot's header byte: the only write the UI makes to it);
    - `statsLifetime(out)` (HEAD's plays and listenedMs, sinceDayNow, dated, counting days, and 4.4's state from RAM).

    They replace `statsShelf()`, `statsHub()` and `statsWeek()`: the pages read the snapshot through `statsSnapshot()` and `statsvisit`'s accessors. `statsTrack()` and the NP members (`navStats_` and the rest) are S4's, under the same rule for the UI's object.
  - *Since V8 (2026-11-21):* no call is added. The Dance tab's release is S3a's (`dancelend::reclaim()`, wired in main.cpp), a refused loan reads No room as any No room does, and the tag scan's wait reads Working, then Waiting, as the other waits do.

## 8. Strings

The rule: the phone app's ARB words where it has them (`i18n/README.md`, "Words"). A text a translation leaves out is English on the device, so a commit with English alone stays green.

### 8.1 From the ARB: no translation owed, but short forms

Phone app `lib/l10n/app_<lang>.arb` at d17c3fa5. All ten languages have the keys.

| Id | English | Room | ARB key:line (en) | Owed short forms | Stage |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| stats.title | Listening stats | Body 204 (entry), Bold 282 (header) | listeningTitle:4397 ("Listening Stats"; the house writes sentence case) | ru (234 px in Body 204) | S3 |
| stats.window_all | All time | Bold 92, else Small | listeningPeriodAll:4465 | es, fr, pt (98, 102, 97 px even in Small); it and ru take the Small fallback | S3 |
| stats.days_n (plural) | {0} days | Bold 92, else Small (tray) | listeningDays:4493 | | S3 |
| stats.plays_n (plural) | {0} plays | Small, inside 264 | listeningPlays:4549 | | S3 |
| stats.recent | Recently played | Body 232, Bold 179 | autoDjRecentlyPlayed:5912 | es, fr, pl, pt, ru (249, 180, 190, 254, 225 px in Bold 179) | S3 |
| stats.empty_title | No plays yet | Title 304 | listeningEmptyTitle:4565 | de, es, fr, it, pt, ru (314-369 px) | S3 |
| stats.today | Today | Bold (section) | listeningToday:4625 | | S3 |
| stats.yesterday | Yesterday | Bold (section) | listeningYesterday:4629 | | S3 |
| stats.plays_label | Plays | Small 100 | listeningTilePlays:4469 | es, ru (103, 110 px) | S3 |
| stats.listened | Listening time | Small 150 | listeningTileTime:4473 | ru (156 px) | S3 |
| stats.shelf_empty | Nothing in this period. | Body 300 (`kListLineW`) | listeningEmptyPeriod:4577 | | S3 |

ARB words keep their translations, with fullwidth punctuation changed to ASCII (`i18n/README.md`, "Characters"). `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md:467` already names d17c3fa5.

The widths are the firmware's VLW fonts (`src/ui/VlwFonts.cpp`: Body Sans 16, Small Sans 13, Bold Sans Bold 16, Title Sans Bold 22, the glyphs' advances summed: MEASURED-HOST). A text that doesn't fit its room fails the check (`i18n/README.md:80-81`). The 18 short forms are written in C19 (drafted, reviewed, fit-passed), each noted in its file's `@@note` as departing from the ARB. Until then the id stays English in that language (C18).

Its one room today is Bold 168, the empty state's button (`:3580-3583`), which Week's No room, Unread and Capped use (7.7). The Try again row on the hub and the shelves (7.4, 7.6) draws it as a row's title, so it gains Body 232: the string tool fits a text to every room listed (`tools/gen_ui_strings.py:310-311`), and nine translations are measured again, none owed anew.
- **The reasons in Week's empty state** (7.7) are its Small line, room 304, which the three new ids' Small 232 already covers.

- **Counts now:** 11 ARB ids and 75 new ones (49 messages and 26 names): **86 ids**. In nine languages **675 texts** are owed for the new ids (27 more, 18 fewer), plus the 18 short forms: **693**, machine-made, with a native review owed.

**Reused, no new text:**
- `format.hours_minutes` (`i18n/en.json:8254-8283`): new rooms (the entry line, the hub's sub, Week's value), and its worst case raised from 99 hours (`:8274`) to 99,999, re-measuring Device info's and the Queue's rooms;
- `format.minutes` (`:8199`), `format.percent` (`:8167`), `empty.shuffle_all` (`:3559`), `toast.no_memory` (`:274`), `library.albums_n`.

**Rules that bite here:**
- A toast splits at its first ": " only when too wide for one Body line (`src/ui/Overlays.cpp:101-107`).
- No fullwidth punctuation in ja and zh.
- Plurals by CLDR (`i18n/README.md`, "Plurals").
- `stats.window_all`: es, fr, pt need short words; it and ru take the Small fallback (8.1). pl's ARB "Cały czas" fits Bold 92 (84 px); the spec measured "Cały okres".

**CJK.** The words (再生, 回, 時間, 分, 今日, 昨日, 月, 日, 播放, 次, 小时, 分钟, 周一-周日, 统计, 跳过) are in JIS X 0208 and GB2312 level 1, the built-in store (`lib/core/CjkGlyphs16.cpp:1-11`): no new glyphs expected. `python tools/gen_ui_strings.py --check` and `test_every_text_has_its_glyphs` decide. Regenerating the store needs the Noto Sans CJK font, which isn't in the repository (`tools/glyph_store.py:85-90`).

**Who translates:** a Fable agent drafts each language and a second reviews it, then the fit pass (`docs/I18N.md:1479-1503`). "The MP3 Player tab" uses the terminal's own name for that tab in each language (terminal `locales/<lang>.yml`). Each file's `@@note` gains a sentence on these texts.

## 9. The stages

### 9.1 Rules, and the dependency diagram

- Host tests stay green at every commit: `pio test -e native`, run from Git Bash on this Windows box (from PowerShell it dies with the MSYS2 DLL clash, 0xC0000139). Builds: `pio run -e core2 -e core2-dio` from PowerShell with MSYSTEM unset.
- A stage stops only at a PR boundary.
- **Device checks run on the spare 32 GB card only, until S5a.** Nothing is played or written on the owner's 1 TB music card before S5a, and S5 needs the owner's word. After S1 merges, the owner's music card boots no main build until S5a passes (the owner flashes main's images onto the board: `docs/RELEASE-0.14.0-CHECKS.md:116-124`), and no release check uses it (`:26`). Silent mode `z` after every boot, before anything plays; never `B`, `P`, `c` or `f` (`docs/RELEASE-0.14.0-CHECKS.md:22-27`). Since `z` keeps logging (O6), every console run adds plays to the card's stats: the spare's stats are test data.
- **The spare card is empty.** 0.14.0's checks formatted it (`docs/RELEASE-0.14.0-CHECKS.md:63-64`) and it is in the PC's reader. S1's device pass first rebuilds it with `tools/synthcard.py` and `tools/synthtransfer.py`, with the owner's go-ahead.
- **Settings are put back** at each device pass's end as `docs/RELEASE-0.14.0-CHECKS.md:110-131` does. S1's seq rule (5.6) keeps that safe for the spare's stats.
- **Fixtures flow one way.** `tools/statsref.py` is the reference. `tools/card_fixtures.py` makes every file in `test/fixtures/card`, and its `--check` fails on any file it didn't make.
- Feature branches never edit `.github/release-notes.md`; 9.2 keeps the lines owed.
- Every stage ends with a review. Its fixes are their own commit, "<Area>: what the review found".

```
S0 --> S1 --> S2 --> S3 --> S4
        |      |      |
        |      |      +--> S6 (the time zone; its terminal half needs nothing here)
        |      +---------> S5b (the owner's card: the lists, after S2)
        +----------------> S5a (the owner's card: the fold, the save, the trim; before any build with S1 boots it)
```

### 9.2 Where it can stop

| Stop after | The listener sees | Useful as |
|---|---|---|
| S0 | Nothing | The contract S1-S4 build to |
| S1 | Nothing on the screen. `/.player/stats.bin` on the card | **Counting starts.** Ship it alone and the history is there when S3 lands. The format is frozen from its first release |
| S2 | Nothing: console only | The lists, by name, on the console |
| S3 | The entry row, the hub, the shelves, Last 7 days | The feature, but the NP sheet's row |
| S4 | The NP sheet's row | The whole design |
| S5a, S5b | — | Checked on the owner's own card across a real sync (S5a gates any release or main build with S1 on that card) |
| S6 | Local days | O4's later half |

**The shortest useful path is S0, S1, S2, S3.**

**Owed to the release commit** (not written into `.github/release-notes.md` by any stage):
- S1:
  - "The player now keeps listening stats on the card (`/.player/stats.bin`): every play on the card, from every player that wrote it, counted from the play log. Nothing on the screen yet."
  - "The log's files are trimmed after a sync only once the stats hold their plays."
  - The downgrade line: "Going back to 0.14.0: it trims a synced card's log without counting those plays, so the stats miss them when the card comes back."
  - The first boot's cost: one fold of the log and one save (S1's device figures). Measured (5.14's "Its device runs"): a full log's first stats boot folds 13,106 slots (about 1.2 s) and saves (2.5-2.7 s); a track started meanwhile waits 4.4-4.8 s, once a card; the next boot compacts (about 1.3 s).
  - Not owed since visit B (2026-11-19): with #63's hole hint a failed stats save on a nearly full card holds the player 98-191 ms (5.3 s before it, B2); the first allocation after a computer filled the card, about 5.5 s once a mount, is #63's uncovered case and main's too, not S1's.
- S3:
  - The screens in a paragraph: the entry row, the hub, the shelves, Last 7 days; Most played opens on All time; days are UTC for now; the first month's states.
  - The strings' provenance: the ARB words at d17c3fa5 in all ten languages, but 18 short forms where they didn't fit (8.1); the rest machine-made in nine, native review owed.
- S4: the NP sheet's first row.
- S6: local days from the computer's time zone; NVS schema 7, nothing to do on the update; going back reads it as nothing (UTC).

### 9.3 Console, one list

| Command | Stage | Does |
|---|---|---|
| `H` | S1 | The state: phase; `stats.bin`'s bytes, saves and minor; HEAD's totals; since and counting days; the rows of each section and their bytes; RAM; the last save (why, ms, bytes). What it reads on the card (`stats.bin`'s header and rows) prints at the next pass the write gate lets through, not behind the fence; the rest at once |
| `Hd` | S1 | The last 7 UTC days: time listened, plays, skips; DAYS by source. Read at the next pass the write gate lets through, not behind the fence |
| `Hm` | S1 | MARK's rows beside ACKS's and the log's highest seq per (deviceId, epoch), from the boot's scan; HEAD's twice and gap |
| `Ht<16 hex>` | S1 | A track's row by path hash (base ⊕ delta), read on the loop at the next pass the write gate lets through, not behind the fence (console only) |
| `Hs` | S1 | Save now, the rotation's path, after the WriteGate as the real saves. Refused during the boot's scan (or its overflow's tries), behind the fence, with the card away, after a partial scan or a changed `stats.bin`, and while the session is behind its log (5.5) |
| `Hr` | S2 | Rank now (the worker): its ms, the records' copy's µs on the loop, the rows read, the lists' sizes |
| `Hl<shelf>[w]` | S2 | A list: hash, id, count, title. Shelf 0 Most played, 1 New finds, 2 Old favourites, 3 Not played yet (its albums), 4 Recently played; Most played's window 0 7 days, 1 30 days, 2 All time (the default) |
| `Hw` | S2 | Rank and walk now: passes, ms, played hashes matched and not, the albums played, the lists' ids |
| `Hp` | S2 | The playing track's facts (base ⊕ TRKD ⊕ the records, as `Ht` prints them; the read's ms on the card worker) |
| `Hp`, more | S4 | Also the NP row's choice (`statsview`'s, built in S3) |
| `uiS` | S3 | The stats view as the UI reads it |
| `uiSh`, `uiSw`, `uiS<shelf>[w]` | S3 | Open the hub, Week, a shelf at a window |
| `uiSx<n>` | S3 | A made-up stats view, look only (as `uil<n>`, `src/main.cpp:1505-1513`): 0 the card's own, 1 a full card, 2 nothing played, 3 no clock, 4 day 5 of counting, 5 folding, 6 no card; as decided (2026-11-20, later): 7 waiting, 8 no room, 9 unread |
| `Hv`, `Hv1` | S3a | A visit's ask, as the hub makes it: the grade Full when Not played yet is open or may be (no clock), else Lists; Full refused, Lists (`Hv1`: Lists forced). Its line: the grade and block, the hold's ms lit or dark with the rank's priority, queued, rank and walk ms, passes and the longest, the turn's and the hand-off's loop ms, `finishRank()`'s whole µs (the seal and the copy-out) and `resolve()`'s; the snapshot's carve, with the largest free block before and after it (whether it came out of the largest; *since V8* the loan: the Dance tab's block, its address and PSRAM's free beside it, or the refusal's cause), and the take's free block above; the waits met before the take and any give-back after it; then the snapshot's bytes, each list's rows and total, the 7 days, the first and newest dated day, Not played yet's count. The snapshot is freed after it (a console ask holds nothing). *Since V8:* released, not freed; the ask also waits for the card worker's tag scan while its memory is carved (`kWhyRest`), and is refused while the Dance tab is up (No room) |
| `Hn`, `Hn1` | S3a | The lean Not played yet ask (the u40 played set, the folders' states, then without them; `Hn1`: without, forced): its grade, block, times and count. *Since V8:* it waits for the tag scan's memory too, and needs no snapshot, so it runs with the Dance tab up |
| `Hu`, `Hu0` | S3a | A held fake visit, for the device's check of call 1: `Hv`'s ask, its snapshot kept as a page keeps it until `Hu0`. Freed at the fence (as a page's), and asked again after the Live and Auto DJ's rejoin at the first pass the gates let through, before anything else is asked (as a visit made after the Live). Each carve's address and bytes printed. *Since V8:* lent and released, not carved and freed; also released when the Dance tab comes up, and not asked again then; each loan's address printed |
| `Hk` | S3a | The held snapshot as a page reads it: bytes, address, key, header (totals, the 7 days, the span, the window), each list's rows with titles (as `Hl`), Not played yet's count and first albums |
| `Hc0`, `Hc1`, `Hc` | S3a | The lit cap (9.7, "The lit hold"): off, on, or which, for this session (RAM; nothing saved). On: UI and console visit asks rank at priority 1 and walk with the dark's passes, lit or not, and a visit's block is given back once its holds reach 3 s in all. Its default is `kLitCap`, a constant, set after run 3a-1: on since V8 (the owner's "Cap on", 2026-11-21) |

- *As decided (2026-11-20, later):* `v`, `n`, `u`, `k` and `c` are free under `H` (`src/app/ListeningStats.cpp:878-917` takes the empty line, `d`, `m`, `t`, `r`, `l`, `w`, `p`, `s`). S2's `Hr`, `Hl`, `Hw`, `Hp` don't change; `Hw` and `Hl3` stay the exact (u64) reference.
- `H` is free at the top level (`src/app/SerialConsole.cpp:226-263`). `E` belongs to the ESP-NOW spike's branch, and `g`'s sub-letters overlap PR #25's `gx`.
- `uiS` (capital) is not `uis` (the scripted swipe, `uis<x0>,<y0>,<x1>,<y1>,<ms>`: its help at `src/main.cpp:1528-1537`, its parser at `:1538-1555`), and Ui's own letters are r, B, v, f, b, c, L, P, T, M (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:2939-3030`).
- *As built (S3a, 2026-11-21; `src/app/StatsLists.cpp`, `command()`):* S3a's rows above, with these departures. (The `H` switch cited above as de4f52f's `:878-917` is `src/app/ListeningStats.cpp:902-955` at S3a.)
  - **`Hv`** releases its snapshot after it only if it borrowed it (until V8: frees, carved). Over a held one (a page's, or `Hu`'s) it rewrites that one whole and keeps it.
  - **`Hv`'s lines:**
    - the loan (since V8): "the snapshot N B in the Dance tab's block at 0x… (4096 B): lent, not carved (PSRAM's free X B before, Y B after)", or, refused, "no block for its snapshot's N B: <cause>" (the Dance tab up, past 18,432 albums, or no block made at the boot). Until V8 the carve: the address, the block, what it took of free, and the largest free block before and after it, out of it or elsewhere by a heap walk;
    - each grade refused;
    - the take: the grade, the block, `sizeof(Ask)`, the u40 rows, the folders and albums, T and its caps, the waits met, lit or dark, and the turn's µs with its own lines left out;
    - each change of the pages' phase;
    - the landing: the hold, lit or dark at the take, the rank and the walk; the visit's holds; the priority; the queued, rank and walk ms, the tracks, the passes and the longest; the loop's pieces in µs;
    - then the snapshot, as `Hk`'s header gives it.
  - **A take that ends without landing** says its own figures ("the take's figures (not landed)").
  - **`Hn`** writes its count into a held snapshot that has landed, else counts from its walk's bits.
  - **`Hu`** asks Full, else Lists. The snapshot is `Hu`'s only when its own ask borrowed it (carved, until V8). Over a page's snapshot it stays the page's (`Hu0`: "isn't Hu's: kept"), and a page's visit over `Hu`'s takes it over.
  - **`Hk`** prints over later passes, a piece at a time while the serial port's TX buffer has room for it: each of the header's three pieces at 768 B free, each row or album at 320 B. The header comes first, then each list's rows, with titles once landed over this library, then Not played yet's first 20 albums. No pass waits on the UART.
  - **`Hc`** alone says the cap's state, as `Hc0` and `Hc1` do after switching it.
  - **The waits** (since V8): the asks' begin line lists the tag scan's memory among their waits (since V10b: "not waited for while the battery floor pauses the scan: the take's decoder-room check then", case (a) as settled), and each wait's line names "the card worker's tag scan (its memory held)".

### 9.4 S0: the contract

No firmware change, no device step.

- **C0: this document.**
- **C1: the frames.** PNG captures (320 x 240) of the nine final frames from the canvas, into `docs/img/listening-stats/`, with a README naming each as this plan does and saying they are browser-font renders. Not AlbumPlays, and none of the A-, B- or C- frames.
- **C2: the format text.**
  - `docs/METADATA.md` 2.12.6: a bullet for `stats.bin` (the queue's commit, the twins `stats.xl1`-`.xl9`, the only-tmp rule with a real check) and the base file (a fresh name, named by `stats.bin`).
  - 2.11's `/.player` row (`:1790`): deleting it loses the stats' history the trims removed; the software never reads the stats files.
  - 2.20.2 "Trimming by ACKS" (`:3034`): a device that keeps stats trims a file only once its stats hold its plays.
  - 2.20.4's "At boot" sentence (`:3112-3122`): the next seq also takes ACKS's highest seq for this device and epoch, and a device that keeps stats its stats' highest (MARK's): a reservation, which the same item allows.
  - 10.1's list (`:8595`).
  - The format itself is this document's 5.2-5.3: no software reads it.
- **C3: the reference and the fixtures.**
  - `tools/statsref.py` (stdlib, imports `tools/playlog.py`): the rules of 4.1, the fold of 5.6 with its dedupe, `stats.bin` and the base byte for byte, ⊕, the rankers of 4.3, the windows, and a `seed` command (writes `plays.jnl` and `plays.1.jnl` of a history: N days of dated plays over a card's `/music` listing, another deviceId, a full log; with `--base <rows>`, also a `stats.bin` and its base of that many rows; for S1-S3's device passes).
  - As built: `statsref.py dump` reads a `stats.bin` (and its base: by default the `stats-<gen>.bin` beside it; with none found, HEAD as read, never a lost base) as the device does; `statsref.py boot DIR --now --acks` runs a boot over a copy of a card's `/.player` (nothing written) and prints what it folds, saves and trims, HEAD, MARK and the lists: the expected values of S1-S3's device passes. `seed CARD` takes `--days` (40) and `--per-day` (25), or `--slots N`, or `--full` (13,106: both files at the cap; `--slots 13102 --device <id>` is run G's log, 4 slots under the cap), `--device`, `--epoch`, `--until`, `--distinct` (5,000), `--base ROWS` (`--base-days`, 365), `--seed`, `--dry-run`, and `--replace` (log files, and with `--base` stats files, already there are refused without it; a twin always is: the log's, `plays.xl1`-`.xl9`, and with `--base` `stats.bin`'s, `stats.xl1`-`.xl9`). The container's frame is `tools/mpwf.py`'s writer, the same bytes as `card_fixtures.build_container`'s.
  - `seed` refuses a card with no `SYNTHCARD.TXT` at its root, or whose `/.mstream` root `tools/synthtransfer.py` didn't write (its root's producer: `tools/synthtransfer.py:26-39`), and, when CARD is a drive's root, the system drive and any file system but FAT32/FAT16 (synthtransfer's `check_drive`, `:1365`); a synthcard tree in a folder, on C: too, passes, as with synthtransfer. It never touches a card without both: seeded plays on the owner's card would be posted to the owner's mStream at the next sync. `--dry-run` lists what it would write.
  - `tools/card_fixtures.py`'s `generate()` writes `test/fixtures/card/stats/`: scenarios (a log, a MARK, ACKS) and their expected `stats.bin`, base and lists. The worked example (5.13) is one. Every verdict of 5.4 has one (`newer`, `corrupt`, the base `base-lost`, `base-other`, `base-unread`, `base-short` and `base-neighbours`), as do 5.12's newer minor (`newer-minor`), a clock a year ahead then corrected (`clock-ahead`) and C8's three partial scans (`partial-open`, `partial-read`, `partial-tail`; the third's live play and `Hs` are a unit test). The cuts of C8 are not modelled: a cut leaves the old commit or the new one, which these files give.
  - `tools/test_statsref.py`, added to `.github/workflows/firmware.yml`'s "Card fixtures" step (`:115-121`), or it never runs in CI.

**Done when:**
```
python tools/card_fixtures.py --check

The stack sums are 11's. After the S1 review's fixes: flash 4,465,943 B (core2-dio 4,465,975 B), built from a tree with changes not yet committed and a longer version string. At a626545 (S1's first gate) it is 4,465,927 B (core2-dio 4,465,959 B). RAM is 59,152 B, `.iram0.text` holds S0's 25 objects, and **IRAM left is 5,157 B**, 4 B under S0's figure. Nothing new is in `.iram0.text`, but `libphy.a(phy_chip_v7_cal.o)`'s `.iram1` is 4,159 B where it was 4,155 (4,455 B before relaxing in both builds).

  S1's gate found the cause (MEASURED-HOST, against 410e2bf built again: ELF 755a9763, C10's): no call in the object changed, but one literal is no longer shared. The object's literal for `phy_chan_gain_table+8` had resolved to the address of `phy_chan_pwr_index`, the COMMON symbol placed next. The linker merges literals of equal value, so `tx_gain_table_set` had loaded it from `ram_set_chan_cal_interp`'s pool. The review's commits added no static byte, but they changed the order in which the linker gives libphy's COMMON symbols their addresses: `bt_wifi_chan_data` now follows `phy_chan_gain_table`. The literal's value is new, so it takes its own 4 B. The review's note had put the 4 B down to the relaxation of the object's calls and where flash code lands: the gate found no call changed.

  GUESS: ld places COMMON symbols in its symbol table's order, so a change that adds or removes global symbols anywhere can move this word again, either way. Whether 11's "IRAM left equals S0's figure" covers this word was the owner's call, settled 2026-11-19: accepted (section 1, call 3).

**Round 1** (2026-11-12 UTC; the owner's Core2 on COM3 with the spare card, silent mode; S1 at 0164e8a, `v0.14.0-46-g0164e8a`; no control run). The runs were adapted to the card's stub tracks (2-5 s FLACs and Opus, MP3s under 1 s): a stub never leaves the 20-30 s the update's safe point wants, nor opens the write gate while it plays, so a pause opened them. Logs in the session's scratch folder (`dev/round1`, `A.log` ... `L.log`).
- **Passed: A, B, C, D, J.** A: no `stats.bin`, the seed folded and saved with its first compaction (generation 1, 14,504 bytes, in 69 ms; the base 653 rows). B: 3 plays, a skip and a stop. C: the 5 slots folded once and saved (generation 2, in 56 ms), nothing new at the next reset. D: the read-back. J: the tmp's size check with the sector cache verifying (`Hs` 91 ms).
- **H, in part.** At the pass a pause opened the gate, `Hs` saved "nothing new" with 4 plays waiting for that gate (H.log 03:12:26.499); they were written after, and a second `Hs` saved them (generation 3, 58 ms). Fixed since: the plays waiting come first (5.5; `statsglue::turnOf()`, `test_the_plays_waiting_come_first`). 0 underruns and breaks, no `[ui] slow pass` in the run. The headphones half wasn't run (silent mode).
- **K, in part.** The boot's save alone: the loop's lowest 3,700 B unused after it, over the house's 2,548 B. G's rotation saves (and the twin's chain, the held tmp) weren't run, G not being run; the DO-low half can't be in this firmware (no fault switch).
- **L, in part.** Four updates, each given back at its fence (62,296 B, MARK's 1,536 B kept) and read again after the rejoin (16-18 s given back; re-reads of 40-86 ms; saves on; L4's give-back saved the FLAC run's plays (61 and 62) first, generation 6 in 52 ms; L4 too had the order at the gate: 63 and 64 were written and saved first, and 65, queued with them behind the Opus run's starts, was written after the give-back into the ring (1 play, folded at the re-read; L.log 03:17:11.182)). At L3's fence the give-back found "nothing to save first" with 3 completed plays queued behind a start's 2 s (L.log 03:16:27.318): they went into the ring and were folded at the re-read, nothing lost, but the save hadn't held them. Fixed since: the update waits for them (5.5; `Glue::updateWaits()`), and the ring holds the Tracker's 32 (5.6; `test_many_short_plays_across_a_give_back`). L4's joined, after the Opus run's buffers, is 167,924 B (`[dj] memory: ... largest 167924 B`), under 245,764 B: the verdict waits for main's control in the same script, not run. The P = 19,478 card and `gb` in a track's last 20-30 s weren't run.
- **The `[ui] slow pass` lines** (about 38 ms each, hundreds in A, C, D and K) come on an empty lit Now Playing ("the page (no frame) ... snapshot"), not from S1's code; main's control in the same script is owed for them too.
- **Not run:** E, F, G, I, and main's controls.
- **Found and fixed after it** (MEASURED-HOST, the host tests and builds): the order at the gate (H and L, above; the fixes' review: a library update's give-back waits out a try the card failed too, else the plays queued behind it went into the ring); the ring at 16 against the Tracker's 32 (L); `H`'s last save with nothing new printing "0 bytes, generation 0" (C.log, D.log; now the generation of the card's `stats.bin` that holds it all); `tools/statsref.py` refusing an ISO time ending in Z (`--now 2026-11-12T03:00:00Z`).

The failed rotation save (an undercount) is proved on the host only (C8). Then the settings put back (9.1).

**PR:** one, C4-C10. **Effort:** 5-7.5 (guess; C8 is 1.5-2.5, the device pass 0.75-1.25).

### 9.6 S2: the map and the rankers

Needs S1. Console only.

- **C11: `lib/core/PathHashWalk.{h,cpp}`**, from `lib/core/AutoDjJoin.cpp:584-647`; the join uses it. No change in the join's output: `test_autodj_join` passes unchanged, and every hash equals `trackPathHash()`.
