Heap figures are compared only with a control in the same script (`docs/AUTODJ.md:1750`): the controls are main at bc29b54 and S1's build, both in the same script.

| Run | Do | Must show |
|---|---|---|
| A | Boot; `Hr`, `Hw`, `Hl0`-`Hl4` | Rank and walk times, idle and with an MP3, dark and lit; `Hr` prints the snapshot copy's µs (one loop pass); the lists equal `statsref`'s over the card's copies |
| B | `D` after the boot, with Auto DJ off and on (`D1`); `D` at the rank's peak, with Auto DJ on | PSRAM free and largest, against the controls; joined at or over 245,764 B where the control is, and within 122,880 B of it; otherwise the ladder of 6.7 |
| C | `gb` stopped, then with an MP3, then with a FLAC; `D`, `Dc`; `D` at the rank's peak, with Auto DJ on | No `[heap] FAILED`; largest against the controls; `Dc`'s lowest over 64 KB; joined at or over 245,764 B where the control is, and within 122,880 B of it; otherwise the ladder of 6.7 |
| D | After C's FLAC, an Opus | Plays, 0 skipped (its 65,536 and 62,464 B blocks fit) |
| E | `Hp` at track starts | The read's ms; the facts equal `Ht`'s |
| F | A reset with Auto DJ on: the join and the walk | Never in one pass (`[ui] slow pass`, the passes' ms) |

**PR:** one, C11-C15. **Effort:** 4-6 (guess; the device A/B 0.5-1).

**The owner's decision (2026-11-20): "A: nothing held"** (section 1). It amends the list above: nothing held between asks and no automatic rank (6.4, 6.8); the fence's drop and redo is a guard, since a library update waits for an ask's block; `Hp` reads by a binary search, with no sparse index kept; the live fold's id isn't asked (no list held to patch). The device runs B and F become: **B** `D` after the boot (Auto DJ off and on), and `D` at the rank's peak (`Hw` asked, `D` while it holds its block) with Auto DJ on; **F** a reset with Auto DJ on, `Hw` asked during the boot's join: the rank waits for the join (its "waits: Auto DJ's join" line), and the walk is never in the join's pass.

**The owner's call on B and C (2026-11-20, after the device pass: "Accept dips, push PR").** B's and C's lines (joined within 122,880 B of the control, at or over 245,764 B where the control is) apply between asks. They don't apply at an ask's own 1-3 s peak, where Auto DJ's join and a library update wait for the ask's block and an ask is refused unless a decoder's buffers still fit beside it. They apply again if S3 holds a page's lists longer than a moment.

**The review (2026-11-20; 9.1)**: two reviewers, read-only, one on the portable core and its tests against statsref, one on the firmware's integration (the loop and the worker, the gates, every exit path, the block's layout). Neither found a definite bug; their risks and nits, each checked in the code, are fixed in "Code: what the review found":
- **A queued step held the play log** (the firmware's reviewer): a step queued for a card worker that never took it (none this boot) kept the stats' saves and every slot back for the session, and behind the boot's walk for as long as it ran. Now saves and slots wait only while the step runs, its input, the records' copy and its files taken at the hand-off (`prepare()`), and a step queued 30 s is given up.
- **`AutoDj::joinPending()` said due with no readable index**, where `stepJoin()` begins nothing: a rank asked then waited for good. Now not due without one.
- **`trackRows()` read the base at the first minor's offset and stride**: a newer minor's base (5.12) would be misread. Now TRKS's own; `test_stats_rank`'s new case, a base of 32 B rows after a 48 B header, fails without it.
- **statsref's rank scenarios picked RCNT's hashes by a set's order**, CPython's (another build could write other fixtures): now sorted; the four `stats/rank/*/stats.bin` and `rank.json` written again (679 files, `--check` up to date).
- **The walk grew Auto DJ's join by 48 B** (Auto DJ's object 3,864 to 3,912 B, a TLSF class more at setup: a resident PSRAM shift, which 5.14 measured S1 out of): the walk now keeps nothing the join keeps, the join 824 B again (the device's ELF), and the map keeps its own index check (its `matches()`, true once Ready too).
- Smaller: `Sparse::span()` with no index gives the whole base; a list that needs the clock is refused at the turn; Hr and Hp don't wait for the index; a walk with no folders' states isn't refused for a later index's folders; the take line names Full, NoRoom and NoBlock; the end's comment says what it does; StatsLists' internal state trimmed (the hold and the printer called, not pointers; the block keeps its bytes).

**The device pass (2026-11-20; the owner's "Yes, device pass"; MEASURED).** The Core2 with the spare card only (`tools/synthcard.py`'s library, seed 1: 19,417 tracks, 1,706 albums; its `stats.bin` generation 9 over the base `stats-00000002.bin`, 19,079 rows, P = 19,075 played hashes), Auto DJ on, silent mode (`z`) after every boot, the screen dark from 24 s after each boot, so every rank ran at priority 1. S2 at 611964d for runs A-E and the first F, then at a5c2950 (the fix below) for F again and the lists again. Main 645679f's CI build is the control, in the same scripts with S2's asks left out. S1 has been in main since #61, so main stands for both of the controls named above. NVS was never written; the music card was never in the board. Logs are in the session's scratch folder (`dev/logs`). Each ask carves one block: `Hr` 80,608 B, `Hl` 121,996 B (269,456 B for Not played yet), `Hw` 285,836 B, `Hp` 12,832 B.
- **A:** `Hr` ranks in 697-1,015 ms on the card worker, idle and with an MP3 (C's: 710-1,019 ms). It queues about 0.5 s first (the input-quiet wait), and the records' copy takes 44-47 µs on the loop (92 µs with 2 records). It reads the base's 19,079 rows, TRKD's 46 and RCNT's 3,950: 506,704 B in all. `Hw` walks 19,417 tracks in 654 ms idle (42 passes, the longest 11.9 ms) and in 898 ms with an MP3 (46 passes, the longest 17.8 ms). All 19,075 played hashes match a track. No `[ui] slow pass`, 0 underruns. **Lit (priority 0) wasn't measured:** no touch or power key is sent.
- **The lists:** all seven equal `statsref`'s over the card's own `stats.bin` and base, copied read-only after the runs (day 323). Most played 7 days, 30 days and All time give 66 rows each, New finds 44, Old favourites 216 and Recently played 216. Not played yet gives 1 album of 1,706: the comparator's first count of 6 had included folders of .m4a and .ogg files, which `LibraryIndex` doesn't index.
- **B:** after the boot, the largest free block is 524,276 B in both builds. At `Hw`'s peak S2 reads 204,788 B idle against main's 557,044 B, and 286,708 B with an MP3 against 638,964 B. The 352,256 B gap is the 285,836 B block plus the caps that close the free block above it. After the ask, the largest is main's again. **Auto DJ off (`D1`) wasn't run:** switching it is a settings command, not sent.
- **C:** after each update (stopped, with an MP3, with a FLAC) and after each `Dc`, the largest free block equals main's to the byte (286,708, 319,476, 401,396 and 286,708 B). Each of the seven joins is placed as main's. `Dc`'s rejoin reads its lowest free at 75,936 B (main 75,992 B), and there is no `[heap] FAILED`. At `Hr`'s peak after each update S2 reads 233,460 B against main's 319,476 B (-86,016 B), then 311,284 B against 401,396 B (-90,112 B), then 286,708 B against 286,708 B (the block went into a smaller free block). After each update `Hw` is refused for room ("with it held no free block takes a decoder's buffers"), as the model (C13) said at P = 19k, and every figure after the refusal is main's.
- **D:** the Opus after C's FLAC update plays its 30 s with 0 underruns in both builds. Free reads 507,376 B against main's 507,432 B, and the largest is 286,708 B in both.
- **E:** `Hp` equals `Ht` for an MP3, a FLAC and an Opus. Each read takes 15.4-19.1 ms on the card worker, with 13-14 base reads (the binary search).
- **F, first (611964d):** `Hw`, asked at 7.8 s, waited for the join and took its block when the join ended (10.8 s). Then its rank queued behind the boot's walk (22.6 s of steps, done at 51.0 s), holding the block, and the 30 s deadline gave the step up at 40.7 s. **Fixed in a5c2950** (6.8: a rank's ask also waits for the card worker's walk and compactions). **F again (a5c2950):** `Hw` waited for the join (8.6 s), then for the walk (10.7 s). It took its block at 50.7 s, once the walk was done, and walked in 1,864 ms (77 passes, the longest 13.1 ms). The walk never ran in the join's pass, and there was no slow pass.
- **PSRAM 56 B under main at every `D`, and at every join's lowest, since setup** (834,780 B against 834,836 B over `setup()`). `CardTasks`, carved in PSRAM at setup (`psramNew`), is 24 B larger with ScanScheduler's ninth job: 2,824 B against 2,800 B (ScanScheduler 432 B against 408 B; gdb on both ELFs). Nothing else is held, and the largest free block is main's at every `D` outside an ask.
- **Against the table's lines:**
  - Met:
    - the lists equal `statsref`'s;
    - every joined figure after an update and its rejoin is main's (so within 122,880 B, and over 245,764 B wherever main is);
    - `Dc`'s lowest is over 64 KB, with no `[heap] FAILED`;
    - the Opus plays;
    - `Hp` equals `Ht`;
    - the walk never runs in the join's pass.
  - **Not met as written, at an ask's own peak:**
    - `Hw`'s block, at 285,836 B, is itself larger than 122,880 B, so `D` at its peak can't come within 122,880 B of main's. It reads 204,788 B idle, under 245,764 B where main's 557,044 B is over it.
    - `Hr`'s peak after the first update reads 233,460 B, under 245,764 B where main's 319,476 B is over it (it is within 122,880 B of main's).
  - Under A each peak lasts 1-3 s: an update and the join wait for it (`statsWait`, `statsHold`), and the ask is refused unless a decoder's buffers fit beside it. The table's lines were written for blocks held between asks.
  - **The owner accepts the dips (2026-11-20, relayed word for word: "Accept dips, push PR").** B's and C's lines apply between asks, where S2 matches main to the byte. They don't apply at an ask's own 1-3 s peak. There, the gate covers what the lines protect (Auto DJ's join and a library update wait for the ask's block), and so does the refusal rule (an ask is refused unless a decoder's buffers still fit beside it). If S3 holds a page's lists longer than a moment, the lines apply again (13's "PSRAM at the ceiling" stays S3's).
- **Not covered:** a lit screen, a real Bluetooth stream or the headphones (silent mode only), Auto DJ off, and the music card (S5a first).
- **The board** was left parked in its ROM bootloader (`read-mac`, "Staying in bootloader"), on main 645679f's CI build, with the spare in. The spare's state is that of the runs' plays and library updates.
- **a5c2950's gate:** it changes `src/app` alone (`CardTasks`, `StatsLists`), which no host test compiles, so the host gate above stands. `pio run -e core2 -e core2-dio` succeeds for both (built with these docs' edits uncommitted, so the version string ends in "-dirty"; the core2 size equals a clean build's). Against main 645679f's CI build:
- core2 is **4,497,439 B (+19,448 B)** and core2-dio 4,497,471 B (+19,448 B): a5c2950 adds 168 B over 72af6b9's tree;
- RAM is 61,488 B (+24 B);
- `.iram0.vectors` and `.iram0.text` are byte for byte main's (IRAM left 5,157 B), with the same `libc_a-*` objects;
- `iram_diet` moves 51 of 51, and `cache_guard`, `flash_guard` and `shadow_guard` are ok.

### 9.7 S3: the Library screens and Last 7 days

Needs S2.

**As decided (2026-11-20, later; section 1's four calls, under "A: nothing held"): two PRs.**
- **S3a** carries the core, the app, the heap model and the console. It proves the memory and timing claims from the console before any screen exists.
- **S3b** carries the screens: the plan's C16-C23, kept below as "S3b's base" and changed by the table in "S3b: the screens".
- S3b starts after S3a's device runs, after the owner has the lit figures if run 3a-1 finds a hold past 3 s, and after the owner has any difference run 3a-4 finds.
- No UI ask is covered by "Accept dips" (1-3 s peaks, all measured dark) until run 3a-1 has measured its hold.
- *As run (2026-11-21; "S3a: the device pass", below):* the runs are done. Run 3a-1 found lit holds past 3 s and run 3a-4 a difference, so S3b waits for the owner's calls on both, and on a third the pass raised (the tag scan's rest).
- *As decided (2026-11-21, later):* the owner settled all three (section 1, word for word): "Borrow Dance's block" for the snapshot, "Cap on" and "Only while held". They are built as V8 and V9 with V8's review ("S3a: the owner's calls as built", below), and S3b waits for their device pass (runs 3a-4 again, 3a-8 and 3a-9) and the owner's word on the review's three cases, *settled 2026-11-22* (section 1: (a) "Don't wait at the floor", built as V10b, 86f1990; (b) and (c) "Keep both as built").
- *As run (2026-11-22; "S3a: V8-V10's device pass", below):* the pass is done on V10b's image (8d56155's). 3a-4 again, 3a-9 and 3a-8's first boot pass. 3a-8's second boot fails its checker's as-main rule: two differences are by design and older than V8, and one, `Hr`'s address 256 B off main's, is modelled only and goes to the owner. S3b waits for that look and for the owner's word on the push and the PR. *Settled (2026-11-22, latest):* "Accept 3a-8". The second boot passes by the owner's call, and the PR is #67.

**S3a: the snapshot.** It lives in `lib/core/StatsVisit`. It is one plain `psramAlloc` block, carved at a visit ask's turn before the take. It is never carved by `new` or `malloc`: at 4,095 B or less those go to internal RAM (`kMallocInternalMax`, #64). It is byte-packed and read with `cardcontract`'s `get16`/`get32`. Each list keeps room for its cap and 16 rows, so the copy-out can drop orphans before cutting to the cap. *As decided (2026-11-21, later; "Borrow Dance's block"; V8):* the snapshot is not carved. It is lent the Dance tab's 4,096 B tracker scratch (`DanceMode::lendScratch()`, `statsvisit::loanOk()`), a PSRAM block `DanceMode` carves at the boot and keeps, so it is still a plain PSRAM block, never internal RAM, with the same layout. `kBound` (4,096 B) is now that block's size too: a library past 18,432 albums gets no loan (No room).

| Part | Rows | A row | Bytes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Header: magic and flags (the grade, dated, the bits present) 4 B; the key, `buildStamp()` u64 and `trackCount()` u32; the ask's time (seconds since 2026, 0 undated) u32, today u16, `Fold::liveRound()` u32; the rows a list, 6 × u16; the totals a list, 6 × u32; firstDay, newestDay, rcntFirstDay, sinceDay, u16 each; the 7 days, each listenedMs u32, plays u16, Auto DJ's plays u16 and skips u16 (70 B); Not played yet's count, albums, the bits' and the directory's offsets, u16 each; Most played's window u8 and 1 B spare; the version u32 | — | — | 160 |
| Most played: 7 days, 30 days, All time | 3 × 66 | id u16, count u16 | 792 |
| New finds | 66 | id u16, count u16 (bits 14-15 the source) | 264 |
| Old favourites | 216 | id u16, count u16, last day u16 | 1,296 |
| Recently played | 216 | id u16, when u24 (minutes since 2026-01-01; 0xFFFFFF undated and this session, 0xFFFFFE undated and earlier) | 1,080 |
| Not played yet: the album bits, then the A-Z directory (a u16 count of clear bits each 64 albums) | — | — | ⌈albums / 8⌉ + 2 × ⌈albums / 64⌉: 268 at 1,706 albums |
| **The block** | | | **3,860 B on the spare** (3,867 B at 1,747 albums); 4,096 B at about 3,200 albums |

- **Ids are u16.** A library past 65,534 tracks gets no ask; none fits PSRAM anyway (11's ceiling is 23k-27k tracks). During the ask the id field holds the row's wanted-set index (at most 695), so the block is served to the pages only once the visit's ask has landed (`snapshot()` null until then). A shelf acts on its rows in place, through the queue's id view (7.6): each list's ids at their stride (4, 4, 6 and 5 B).
- **Past about 3,200 albums** (11's ceiling is about 2,500), Old favourites and Recently played give up their last rows to stay at 4,096 B.
- **Saturation:** counts saturate at 65,535 (New finds' at 16,383), and `when` at 2057.
- *As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):*
  - **The header's fields** take 154 B; the other 6 B are zero.
  - **The ask's time** is a row's time (`statsfold::timeOf()`: seconds since 2026-01-01 plus 1; 0 undated), not plain seconds. Plain seconds would read 2026-01-01 00:00:00 as undated, and `when` reads a row's minutes as (time − 1) / 60.
  - **The trim** starts at 3,217 albums: the block is 4,096 B at 3,216. Old favourites and Recently played have given up all 216 rows from 18,361 albums, and from 18,433 albums the block passes 4,096 B.

**S3a: the grades.** `statsvisit::layout()` computes each grade's layout, reusing regions once they are dead. Each grade is one block at the top through S2's hold (`statshold::take()`: its caps, a decoder's room and the yield unchanged).
- **The parts**, as 9.6's device pass and the code give them:
  - the ask's head (`sizeof(Ask)`): 1,488 B, ESTIMATED (S2's 1,376 B plus about 112 B of new fields: the totals 24 B, the 7 days 70 B, the span and RCNT's first day 6 B, and the handles of the records' DAYS rows and the u40 set). `Hv`'s line prints the true figure, and every grade below moves with it byte for byte;
  - the work memory (`statsfile::kWorkBytes`): 11,456 B;
  - the Ranker: 16,704 B (696 rows of 24 B);
  - RCNT's window: 49,632 B (4,136 rows of 12 B);
  - the records' copy: 2,080 B, 40 × (TRKD 24 + RCNT 12 + DAYS 16); 960 B for TRKD alone;
  - the folders' states: 24,784 B, and the album bits: 224 B (the spare's 2,478 folders and 1,706 albums, used in both columns);
  - the wanted set: 8,352 B (696 rows of 12 B in one region: `Wanted::rowsCap`);
  - the u40 played set: ⌈rows × 5⌉ to 16, with rows = HEAD's baseRows + TRKD's rows + 40, as S2 counts them.
- **One TRKD assumption, two columns:**
  - the spare as measured: base 19,079 + TRKD 46 + 40 = 19,165 rows, 95,840 B;
  - P = 19,478 with TRKD 1,365: 20,883 rows, 104,416 B.

| Grade | When | Layout | Spare | P = 19,478 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Full** | A visit with Not played yet open (or, with no clock, perhaps open) | head + A [the Ranker, the work memory, the records; at `finishRank()` the wanted set in A's tail, then the folders and bits at its head, over the dead Ranker] + PW [RCNT's window; once finished, the u40 played set] | **130,688 B** (A 33,360 B, PW 95,840 B) | **139,264 B** |
| **Lists** | A visit with Not played yet closed (the first month), or Full refused | head + [the Ranker, the work memory, RCNT's window, the records; then the wanted set and the folders] | **81,360 B** | 81,360 B |
| **Lists, no clock** | The same with the clock unset: no window room (`Windows::add()` returns first, `lib/core/StatsRank.cpp:203-204`) | head + max(Ranker + work + records, folders + wanted) | **34,624 B** | 34,624 B |
| **Not played yet, lean** | Not played yet's Try again after a visit that took Lists because Full was refused (7.6); `Hn` | head + [the work memory, the TRKD records; then the folders and bits] + the u40 played set. The played-set-only rank (V1): no Ranker, no offers, RCNT and DAYS not read, no window | **122,336 B** | **130,912 B** |
| **Not played yet, no folders** | Lean refused; `Hn1` | head + [the work memory, the TRKD records; then the bits] + the played set. The walk hashes whole paths, about 3.3 times slower (`lib/core/PathHashWalk.h:19-22`) | **109,744 B** | **118,320 B** |
| S2's `Hw`, u64 (the reference) | `Hw` | 9.6; S2's regions (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:279-300`) with this table's rows and the spare's folders | 285,836 B (10 chunks) | 302,220 B (11 chunks of 2,048 hashes for 20,883 rows; 6.5's 289,948 B is its own model: ten chunks, at most 20,480 rows) |
| The lean ask with u64 (not built; for the record) | — | head + 25,008 B + rows × 8 | 179,824 B | 193,568 B |

At TRKD's forced-compaction cap (4,096 rows: 23,614 rows in the set), Full is 152,928 B, the lean ask 144,576 B and no folders 131,984 B. At P = 2,214 the window outweighs the played set: Full is 84,480 B.

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):* the ask's head is 1,600 B on both ELFs. V4 first built it at 1,704 B, and 6ea01b0 trimmed its bookkeeping. A `static_assert` in `StatsLists::begin()` holds it to `statsvisit::kAskHeadBytes`, which the heap model reads. Every grade is 112 B over the table:

| Grade | Spare | P = 19,478 | TRKD's cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| Full | 130,800 B | 139,376 B (P = 2,214: 84,592 B) | 153,040 B |
| Lists | 81,472 B | 81,472 B | — |
| Lists, no clock | 34,736 B | 34,736 B | — |
| Lean | 122,448 B | 131,024 B | 144,688 B |
| No folders | 109,856 B | 118,432 B | 132,096 B |

On the host `statsfile::kWorkBytes` is 11,488 B against the device's 11,456 B (5.14's R4). So where the work memory sets a grade's room (Lists with a clock, and No folders), the host's grades read 32 B more again. `test_stats_visit` checks `layout()` against the table at its 1,488 B head.

**S3a: the fit**, by the take's rule B ≤ (L − 133,047 − T) × 32/33, with T about 5.4 KB after an update. This is MODELLED from S2's device pass. T varies: `Hr`'s take once went into a smaller free block (9.6).

The snapshot is carved first, as a plain block (3,860-4,096 B). TLSF's good fit serves it from the smallest free class that holds it, so it comes out of the largest free block only when no smaller one takes it. When it does, L reads one class less: 8,192 B from 262,144 B up, 4,096 B under. Each cell gives the margin both ways: the snapshot elsewhere / out of L. *Since V8 ("Borrow Dance's block"):* the snapshot is lent a block carved at the boot, so it never comes out of a free block, and each cell's first figure (elsewhere) is the one that applies.

**Near the line** means a margin within one class of L, either way. The rule can't call those cells. L is what TLSF reports, a class floor less 12 B (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.cpp:313-316`), so the block itself is up to a class larger. The take's own check also reads the block that is left as a floor, against 133,047 B (`:368`; `carvedAtMost()` at `:106`: 128,023 B + a thirty-second + 1 KB). Run 3a-3 decides them.

| L, the largest free block | B at most (elsewhere / out of L) | Full | Lists | Lean | No folders |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 524,276 B (after the boot, 9.6's B; MEASURED) | 374,137 / 366,193 B | yes | yes | yes | yes |
| 401,396 B (after the FLAC update; MEASURED) | 254,980 / 247,037 B | yes | yes | yes | yes |
| 319,476 B (after the MP3 update; MEASURED) | 175,543 / 167,599 B | yes, at TRKD's cap too | yes | yes | yes |
| 286,708 B (after the stopped update, and after `Dc`; MEASURED, the device's worst) | 143,768 / 135,824 B | the spare yes (13,080 / 5,136 B, the second near). P = 19,478: yes by 4,504 B, near / no by 3,440 B, near: Lists. At TRKD's cap no | yes | the spare yes (21,432 / 13,488 B). P = 19,478 yes (12,856 / 4,912 B, the second near). At TRKD's cap no (808 B short, near / 8,752 B) | yes (at TRKD's cap 11,784 / 3,840 B, the second near) |
| 270,324 B (the model, an Opus kept) | 127,880 / 119,937 B | no (the spare 2,808 B short, near / 10,751 B): Lists | yes | the spare yes by 5,544 B, near / no by 2,399 B, near. P = 19,478 no (3,032 B short, near / 10,975 B) | yes (P = 19,478: 9,560 / 1,617 B, the second near) |
| 249,844 B (the model) | 108,021 / 104,049 B | no: Lists | yes | no | no (the spare 1,723 B short, near / 5,695 B): "Not enough memory right now" |
| 180,212 B or less (the model's Opus histories) | 40,499 / 36,527 B or less | no | no; without a clock yes (5,875 / 1,903 B, the second near) | no | no: every ask but Lists without a clock refused, `Hr`'s 80,608 B too |

- **At every point the board measured after an update,** a visit takes Full on the spare, with 5,136 B or more to spare either way. At P = 19,478 and 286,708 B it takes Full only while the snapshot stays out of the largest free block, by 4,504 B, near the line. Otherwise it takes Lists, and Not played yet's Try again makes the lean ask, which fits by 4,912 B, near the line too. So call 3's "fits at every point measured on the board after an update" holds for the lean ask, at P = 19,478 within a class of the line.
- **The model's lowest points** refuse Not played yet. The model's points aren't the device's (13).
- **Margins:** the near cells rest on T, the head's estimate and where the snapshot lands. Run 3a-3 reads all three: `Hv`'s line gives the largest free block before and after the snapshot's carve, and the take's free block above. (Since V8 there is no carve: the loan's line gives PSRAM's free beside it.)

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21; MODELLED: V5's `test_an_s3_ask_in_a_reduced_room`, fc5bf26).* The 286,708 B row in a model heap:
- the raw block at its class's floor, 286,720 B;
- the rest of free in blocks under the snapshot's class, with one free block of the snapshot's size (elsewhere) or none (out of L);
- the spare's parts and the 1,600 B head;
- each grade asked alone through the hold with the snapshot held.

Each cell gives the margin over 133,047 B, elsewhere / out of L, with T in brackets:

| Rows | Full | Lists | Lean | No folders |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| P = 19,478 (20,883) | +6,205 (4,088) / +2,109 (1,584) | +67,645 (4,088) / +63,549 (3,376) | +14,397 (8,184) / +10,301 (5,296) | +26,685 (8,184) / +22,589 (4,656) |
| TRKD's cap (23,614) | −6,083 (4,088) / −10,179 (3,504) | +67,645 (4,088) / +63,549 (3,376) | +2,109 (4,088) / −1,987 (2,992) | +14,397 (4,088) / +10,301 (2,352) |

Across the class (128 raw sizes, 64 B apart), each grade is taken at all 128 or at none. The one exception is the lean ask at TRKD's cap out of L, taken at 66 (No folders at the other 62).

Where the model and the table above differ:
- **P = 19,478, Full, out of L.** The table says refused: 3,440 B short, or 3,552 B at this head. The model takes it at every raw size. Its spacers leave T at 1,584 B, and given back they are a free block of 137,860 B, reported 135,156 B: the class floor takes 2,692 B, where the rule's 32/33 takes a whole thirty-second.
- **TRKD's cap, Lean, elsewhere.** The table says 808 B short, or 920 B at this head. The model takes it (+2,109 B).
- **TRKD's cap, Lean, out of L.** The table says 8,752 B short, more than a class, so not near. The model has it near: 1,987 B short at the floor, and taken at 66 raw sizes.
- **Near cells the model takes at every size:** P = 19,478's Lean out of L (4,912 B in the table) and TRKD's cap's No folders out of L (3,840 B).

So the rule, with T fixed at 5.4 KB, is pessimistic in this heap. At P = 19,478 a page's visit takes Full either way, so "Lists, then Not played yet's Try again" is reached there by `Hn` alone. The 249,844 B row agrees with the model (`test_an_s3_ask_in_a_model_heap`, the yardstick's library, an Opus kept: 249,844 B after each update). At P = 19,478 the visit takes Lists after each update, and the lean ask and No folders are refused. Run 3a-3 decides the device's cells.

**S3a: the commits.** Each comes with its host tests: Git Bash for the tests, PowerShell with MSYSTEM unset for the builds (9.1).
- **V1, `lib/core/StatsRank`** (`test_stats_rank`; `tools/statsref.py`, `tools/test_statsref.py` and the fixtures):
  - **The read's order** (6.8): `rank()` reads RCNT and the records' RCNT rows first, finishes the window into the Ranker (`Windows::finish()`), then streams TRKS ⊕ TRKD ⊕ the records' TRKD.
  - **DAYS:** `Input::days` and `daysRows` carry the records' DAYS rows (`Fold::Cursor(kDays)`, copied at the hand-off with the others). The report gains `Report::days[7]`, `firstDay`, `newestDay` (`kUnsetDay`: none) and `rcntFirstDay`.
  - **Counts:** `Ranker::total(List)` counts every `offer()` (6 × u32).
  - **The u40 set:** `statsrank::Played40`, with its rows, `n`, `lost`, `at(i)` and `contains(hash)` on the top 40 bits, beside `Memory::played40`. The rank fills one set or the other: S2's `Played` stays for `Hw` and `Hl3`.
  - **No clock:** a null `Memory::windows` is allowed while undated.
  - **The played-set-only rank** (the lean ask's): with `Memory::lists` null there is no Ranker and nothing is offered (`ranker.begin()` and `ranker.track()` skipped: today `rank()` always calls them, `lib/core/StatsRank.cpp:365`, `:427`); RCNT and DAYS aren't read and no window is begun, dated or not (today a dated rank with no window room ends as `Outcome::Room`: `Windows::add()` returns false at its cap, `:204-205`, `:457-463`). Only TRKS ⊕ TRKD ⊕ the records' TRKD are streamed, into the u40 set. `StatsLists::prepare()` then copies the TRKD records alone (960 B; today it copies RCNT's after them: `src/app/StatsLists.cpp:376-399`), and S3's DAYS rows not at all. `test_stats_rank` pins its played set equal to the full rank's on every fixture, clock set and unset.
  - **statsref** gains `totals()`, `days7()` with the span, and `played40()`. `card_fixtures.py` writes the new expectations (`stats/rank.json`), and `--check` is clean.
- **V2, `lib/core/StatsMap`** (`test_stats_map`): `Map::begin()` over a `Played40`, and `Wanted::rowsCap` (a region for fewer rows than a chunk's 1,365). **`Wanted::seal()`** becomes an in-place heap sort over the chunked rows, as the save's sort is (`__adjust_heap`: 11's loop-stack row), then the same dedup. Today it is an insertion sort (de4f52f's `lib/core/StatsMap.cpp:26-46`), run on the loop in `finishRank()` (de4f52f's `src/app/StatsLists.cpp:513-520`): the lists' 674-696 rows come in rank order, effectively random by hash, so about n²/4, 113,000-121,000 inner steps of a byte-wise `get64`, a `row()` division and a 12 B copy in PSRAM, ESTIMATED tens of ms in one pass. S2 never timed it (`[ui] slow pass` times `Ui::loop` alone). A heap sort is about 2n log2 n, about 13,000 steps. `test_stats_map` pins the sealed set equal to the insertion sort's on every fixture and on reversed and equal-hash inputs.
- **V3, `lib/core/StatsVisit`** (new; `test_stats_visit`), and **`lib/core/StatsFold`'s two getters** (`test_stats_fold`):
  - `statsvisit::Snapshot` (the table above) and `bytesFor(albums)`.
  - `write(ranker, wanted, report, liveRound, snap)`, at `finishRank()`: every kept row straight into the snapshot, its wanted-row index (u16) in the id field and its facts packed; the totals, the 7 days and the span from the report. It clears the header's flags first and writes every field, so a block an earlier try left part-written (a yield, Unread) is rewritten whole. Nothing reads it before `resolve()`: `snapshot()` is null until the visit's ask lands.
  - `phaseOf(why, outcome, wait, waitedMs, capped)`: the page's phase from the ask's state (the API's list below). Pure, so `test_stats_visit` checks every case: each of S2's whys, each Rank-lane wait and each yield after the take gives Working, then Waiting from 3 s, uncounted; every read fault (Io, Changed, Damaged, Base) gives Unread and nothing else does; NoRoom, NoBlock and `Room` at every grade give NoRoom; the cap gives Capped. `StatsLists` calls it; no host build covers `StatsLists` itself.
  - `resolve(wanted, snap)`, after the walk: each index becomes `wanted.idAt()`; `kNoId` and `kTwoTracks` rows are dropped; each list is compacted and cut to its cap; the totals lose the orphans dropped among the kept rows.
  - `notPlayed(map, snap)` (the bits and the directory), `albumAt(k)` and `rowAt(list, i)`.
  - `layout(grade, rows, foldersBytes, albums, dated)`: each region's offset and the block's bytes. It is the one arithmetic for `StatsLists`, the console and the model.
  - `Fold::sinceDayNow()` (HEAD's sinceDay, else `firstPlayDay_`: `lib/core/StatsFold.h:450`) and `Fold::liveRound()` (`round24(loadedRound_ + 2)`, from `:448`).
- **V4, the firmware.** Files: `src/app/StatsLists.{h,cpp}`, `src/app/ListeningStats.{h,cpp}`, `src/app/CardTasks.{h,cpp}` (the wait handed over), `lib/core/ScanScheduler.h` (`yieldNow()`), `src/app/PlayHistory` (the turn, and the free points' calls) and `src/main.cpp` (the fence's count). No host build covers `src/app`: the builds and the device runs do, and `phaseOf()` (V3) and `yieldNow()` (`test_scan_scheduler`) are host-tested.
  - **The asks:** the typed asks and their API (below).
  - **The pre-turn gate** (every UI ask, and `Hv`, `Hn` and `Hu`, which ask as a page does): S2's gates (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:198-226`), and the Rank lane's own waits. S2's gates hold only for the stats off, the boot's scan, a held tmp, an update asked or the index, the card, Auto DJ's join and the worker's walk or compaction. The Rank lane also waits for input in the last 0.5 s, the ring below half, an underrun in the last 30 s, a decode pass over 40 ms in the last 5 s, a track change, a seek and Bluetooth (`yieldOf()`, `lib/core/ScanScheduler.cpp:145-157`, checked before a rank is handed out: `:222-225`), and for a lit list moving (`listHolds()`). Without them in the gate, a block taken while one of them holds would sit queued while it lasts: an underrun would keep a visit's block about 5 s in every 5.5 s for 30 s, with Auto DJ's join and a library update waiting on `listsHold()` all that time (`src/main.cpp:3374`, `:3648`). So each pass `CardTasks` hands `StatsLists` the scheduler's wait over that pass's `In`, as it hands `workerAhead()` (`src/app/CardTasks.cpp:274`): `ScanScheduler::yieldNow(in)`, `yieldOf()` made public, or the lit list's `List`. 1 B of `StatsLists`' `.bss`; `CardTasks` doesn't grow. No block is taken while it isn't None. The ask waits in Asked, holding nothing: Working, then Waiting from 3 s. *As decided (2026-11-21, later; "Only while held"; V8):* also the card worker's tag scan while its memory is carved (`statsvisit::kWhyRest`, 128, after S2's gates and the walk's: `CardTasks` hands `restAhead()` each pass, as `workerAhead()`; not memory no step gives back, `CardJobs::scanStuck()`), for S3a's asks only.
  - **The snapshot at the turn:** carved (*since V8*, lent: the Dance tab's block) before the take, if none is held (a visit's; a re-ask reuses the held one, which `write()` rewrites whole). The lean ask writes into the held snapshot only at its landing (`notPlayed()`, on the loop), so the lists stay served meanwhile. The grades are tried in order. With every grade refused, the snapshot is freed if this ask carved it (since V8, released if it borrowed it), and the phase is No room. A refused take leaves no trace: NoBlock asks the largest free block first, and NoRoom gives the block back (`lib/core/AutoDjJoin.cpp:317-320`, `:368-376`).
  - **`finishRank()`:** the wanted set is built in A's tail and sealed (V2's heap sort); then `statsvisit::write()`; then the folders and bits at A's head, and the walk. After the walk, `resolve()` and `notPlayed()`, and the block is given back in that pass.
  - **The hold's bounds** for UI asks. Each of these gives the block back and puts the ask back in Asked (Waiting), with no count:
    - a wait that comes between the take and the hand-off (any of the gate's): given back in the pass that sees it, so a block never queues behind a wait;
    - the walk skipping passes for 1 s (a lit list moving, the card held: `:582`): `map.release()`, given back;
    - S2's 30 s worker give-up (`:37`, `:451`), which the gate now leaves for a worker that never comes.

    Once taken, a UI ask's block is held for the hand-off (a pass or two), the rank (one step, never cut: 0.70-1.02 s dark, lit unmeasured) and the walk (lit: up to 4 ms a pass, about 200 tracks at the dark's rate of 19,417 in 42 passes, so about 100 passes, ESTIMATED 1.5-3 s; unmeasured). Each take's hold, and the visit's sum over its takes, is on the ask's line. Unread is only for Io, Changed, Damaged and Base. *As run (2026-11-21, run 3a-1; MEASURED):* lit, Full's rank took 2,539-4,478 ms and its walk 1,023-1,595 ms in 103-124 passes (the longest pass 5.7-13.8 ms), and with the cap off every lit ask's hold was 2,681-6,104 ms (Full's 3,587-6,104 ms; the visits' summed lines up to 6,155 ms) ("S3a: the device pass", below).
  - **The walk while lit:** slices of 100 tracks, 4 ms a pass, none in a pass that handled input. Dark, as S2's. With the lit cap on, lit as dark.
  - **The lit hold** (below): its instrumentation, `kLitCapMs` and the cap's form, switched by `kLitCap` and `Hc0`/`Hc1`.
  - **The free points:** `release()`; `drop()` frees the snapshot, its bytes in the fence's line and in `alsoFreed` (`src/main.cpp:3635`); `ending()` frees it too. *Since V8 ("Borrow Dance's block"):* the release points are the same, each dropping the pointer and freeing nothing, plus the Dance tab coming up (`dancelend::reclaim()`). `drop()` counts the snapshot's heap bytes, 0 (`StatsLists::snapshotHeapBytes()`), so the fence's `[lib]` line names the listening stats only for an ask's block, and `PlayHistory::statsHeldBytes()` adds 0 to `alsoFreed` (`src/main.cpp:3480`, `:3654-3656` at V10). `listsHold()` stays the ask's block alone (`holds()`, `src/app/StatsLists.h:284`): a held snapshot doesn't hold Auto DJ's join or a library update.
  - **Console:** `Hv`, `Hv1`, `Hn`, `Hn1`, `Hu`, `Hu0`, `Hk`, `Hc0`, `Hc1` (9.3).
- **V5, the heap model's S3 half** (`test/test_autodj_join`): the tests below. The held snapshot's steps (A4) run inside `test_the_listening_stats_across_updates`, as more of its ways, since that case alone is registered in the `-DSTATS_WIDE` build (`test/test_autodj_join/test_autodj_join.cpp:6419-6466`: every other `RUN_TEST` sits inside `#if !defined(STATS_OLD_DECODERS) && !defined(STATS_WIDE)`). A case of its own would pass the wide command without running.
- **V6, the review's fixes**: "Code: what the review found" (9.1).
- **V7, docs:** ARCHITECTURE's Listening stats bullet (the visit's ask, the snapshot, the grades, the gate's waits); README's console table (`Hv`, `Hn`, `Hu`, `Hk`, `Hc`); this plan's "As built".

**S3a: the ask's API for the UI.** S3b calls it through `UiHost`, and S3a's console calls the same functions:
```
enum class UiAsk : uint8_t { Visit, NotPlayed };
enum class Phase : uint8_t { Idle, Working, Waiting, Ready, NoRoom, Unread, NoCard, Capped };
enum class Outcome : uint8_t { None, Ok, NoRoom, NoBlock, Changed, Io, Damaged, Base, Cancelled };
bool ask(UiAsk a);     // queued (Asked); behind a console ask under way; dropped
                       // at once (NoCard) with the card away or held
void cancel();         // Asked: dropped; queued with its block: given back; on the
                       // worker: its result dropped at kDone (a step is never cut:
                       // about 1 s at most); walking: map.release(), given back that pass
void release();        // cancel(), and the snapshot freed
Phase phase() const;  uint8_t why() const;  Outcome outcome() const;
uint16_t version() const;  // +1 at each phase change and each landing
bool news();               // the version moved since the UI last asked (1 B)
const statsvisit::Snapshot* snapshot() const;  // the held one once the visit's ask
                                                // has landed; else null
void setWindow(uint8_t w); // Most played's window into the held snapshot's header
```
- **Phase from why** (S2's bits), by `statsvisit::phaseOf()` (V3):
  - 1 (stats off): Idle, and the entry row is absent;
  - 2 (the boot's scan): Working;
  - 16 (the card away or held): the ask cancelled and the snapshot freed (`release()`), NoCard. It isn't left in Asked, where it would run by itself once the card came back; the page offers Try again;
  - 4, 8, 32 and 64, and the Rank lane's waits (the gate): Working for 3 s, then Waiting (*as decided, 2026-11-21*, "Only while held": 128 too, the tag scan's memory);
  - a wait after the take, given back: Waiting, uncounted;
  - NoRoom, NoBlock or `Outcome::Room` at every grade: NoRoom;
  - Io, Changed, Damaged, Base: Unread;
  - the lit cap reached (cap on): Capped.
- **`StatsLists`' `.bss`** holds the phase, why, outcome, the UI ask's kind, the version, the news byte, the snapshot's pointer, the held fake's flag, the wait's start, the Rank lane's wait (1 B), the cap's switch (1 B) and the visit's summed hold (4 B). It is at most 32 B over S2's, measured at the gate against main's `.dram0.bss` and counted against G5's real-stream margin (7,756 B; section 1).

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21; `src/app/StatsLists.h`, `lib/core/StatsVisit.h`):* the API as above, but for these lines:
```
enum class Outcome : uint8_t { None, Ok, NoRoom, NoBlock, Changed, Io, Damaged, Base, Cancelled,
                               Room, CardAway };
bool ask(UiAsk a, bool cardGone);  // false: dropped: the stats off; the card gone (No card, the
                                   // snapshot released); NotPlayed with no landed snapshot
void release(const char* why);     // `why`: its [listen] line's words
uint16_t version() const;  // +1 at each phase change, each landing, each release of the snapshot, and
                           // when a visit asked again over a served snapshot rewrites it (write())
size_t snapshotBytes() const;      // the held block's room, kBound (0: none): write(), the albums' check
size_t snapshotHeapBytes() const;  // the heap's bytes it holds: 0, lent (drop()'s count, main.cpp's alsoFreed)
void restAhead(bool held);         // CardTasks' word each pass: the tag scan holds its memory (kWhyRest)
```
- *Since V8 (2026-11-21):* the lines above are V8's: "freed" became "released", `snapshotBytes()` was split in two (until V8 it was the carved block's bytes, which `alsoFreed` counted), and `restAhead()` is new. Beside the API: `statshold::borrow(n)` (the loan: main.cpp's, `DanceMode::lendScratch()`), `statshold::danceUp()` (a refused loan's cause), `dancelend::reclaim()` (`DanceMode::setActive(true)`'s call: `statsRelease("the Dance tab")`), `statsvisit::loanOk(exists, danceActive, n)` (pure, host-tested), `statsvisit::kWhyRest`, and `CardJobs::scanHeld()` and `scanStuck()`. `statshold::largestFree()` went with the carve.
- **`snapshot()`** is null from a re-ask's `write()` to its landing, so a page reads it at each use and keeps no pointer.
- **`PlayHistory`** forwards the calls for S3b's `UiHost`: `statsAsk()` (`cardGone` from main.cpp's `cardHeld` and the card away), `statsCancel()`, `statsRelease()`, `statsSnapshot()`, `statsPhase()`, `statsVersion()`, `statsNews()`, `statsSetWindow()` and `statsHeldBytes()`.
- **`news()`** compares the version's low byte.
- **`Room` and `CardAway`** are V3's. `Room` is the rank's, which every grade's refusal reads as; the lean ask's u40 set short of its room ends there too. `CardAway` is an ask the card dropped or cancelled.
- **`phaseOf(why, outcome, wait, waitedMs, capped, served)`** takes a sixth argument, `served`: the landed snapshot still held.

*As built (S3a), the phase from why:*
- **A wait after the take**, its block given back, is the gate's wait again: Working, then Waiting from 3 s, counted from its own start and uncounted. This is the V3 bullet's and the tests' reading, not the list's "Waiting" at once (4.4's Waiting is "held past 3 s"): a tap's 0.5 s Input wait after a take would otherwise flash "Waiting for the card…".
- **64** also holds a visit for 30 s after the card worker's give-up (no step of its ask in 30 s: `kStalled`, `statsvisit::stallHolds()`). It holds nothing meanwhile (Working, then Waiting), and the ask is taken again once the 30 s are up, with no count and no end. The rule lives in `lib/core/StatsVisit.h` (`kStallMs`, `stallHolds()`, `waitStartMoves()`): while stalled, the wait's start stays the give-up's until the take (cf72100).
- **128** (V8; the owner's "Only while held"): the card worker's tag scan holds its memory (`CardJobs::scanHeld()`, handed by `CardTasks` as `restAhead()` through `statsvisit::restHolds()`: not while `scanStuck()`, nor, since V10b, while the battery floor pauses the scan, `ScanScheduler::batteryLow()`). The visiting asks only (the UI's Visit and NotPlayed, `Hv`, `Hv1`, `Hn`, `Hn1`, `Hu`); S2's `Hr`, `Hl`, `Hw` and `Hp` don't wait for it. `gateOf()` gives it after S2's gates and the walk's 64, before the 30 s stall (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:466`). Working, then Waiting from 3 s, uncounted, the wait's start kept while it holds (`waitStartMoves()` false, as for every why); a queued visit it meets gives its block back. Its line: "the card worker's tag scan (its memory held)".
- **16**: the ask is stopped with `Outcome::CardAway` and the snapshot freed (since V8, released). The phase is No card until the next ask, even once 16 has gone, so the page's Try again shows once the card is back. An ask made with the card gone is dropped at once the same way. A remount's key release gives No card too.
- **A landing** reads Ready only while its snapshot is served (`served`), else Idle. Cancelled and the stats off read Idle.
- **A page's ask waiting behind a console ask** reads Working.
- **A page's ask during a remount's re-read of `stats.bin`** waits as for the boot's scan (2: Working), not dropped as with the stats off.

*As built (S3a), `.bss`:* `StatsLists` is 44 B, against S2's 24 B. It holds:
- the snapshot's pointer (4 B);
- the version (u16), the Rank lane's waits met (u16), the visit's holds (u16 ms, saturating);
- the news byte, the last phase, why, the outcome, the page's ask waiting;
- one flags byte: the UI's, forced, `Hu`'s fake, the cap's switch, capped, carved, stalled, `Hu`'s ask;
- the Rank lane's wait, and the visits past the cap;
- `Hk`'s two bytes, in its tail padding.

`.dram0.bss` is 16 B over main's (`ListeningStats` 112 to 128 B, `PlayHistory` 200 to 216 B).

*Since V8:* S2's walk flag (`workerAhead_`, a bool) became a bits byte, `ahead_` (`kAheadWalk` 1, `kAheadRest` 2), so `StatsLists` stays 44 B, held there by `static_assert(sizeof(StatsLists) == 44)` (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:294`); the flags byte's "carved" bit (`kCarved`) marks the snapshot this ask borrowed. The loan's wiring is free functions in main.cpp (`statshold::borrow()`, `statshold::danceUp()`, `dancelend::reclaim()`), so `DanceMode` keeps main's 2,008 B, and `.dram0.bss` is still 16 B over main's (36,392 B against 36,376 B).

**S3a: the snapshot's life.**
- **Carved** at a visit ask's turn, before the take, if none is held.
- **Freed** (`release()`), each time with a `[listen]` line saying why:
  - leaving the stats pages: Back to Artists, the Library tab tapped to its root, another segment;
  - another tab, Now Playing among them;
  - the dark (`Ui::setDark(true)`, `src/ui/Ui.cpp:2348`);
  - the fence (`StatsLists::drop()`, and `Ui::libraryUpdating()` at `:748`);
  - a Live or a rebuild (`Ui::libraryChanged()`, `:731`);
  - `browse` (`:761`);
  - a remount whose index no longer matches the key (checked at each `update()`: a remount doesn't reset the tab, `src/main.cpp:3237-3284`);
  - the card away or held;
  - the stats off;
  - the orderly end.

  At each free made while a stats page is attached (the dark, the card, the stats off, a remount's key), `Ui` attaches the page again at 0 rows in the same call, its bar folded into its ref and any sheet closed (7.2).
- **Asked:**
  - entering the stats pages (the hub's push from the entry row; S4's jump);
  - coming back to them from elsewhere: the Library tab shown again with a stats page in its stack;
  - a wake on a stats page;
  - Try again (after No room, Unread, Capped, or the card back), Not played yet's lean ask among them (7.6).
- **Not asked:**
  - a shelf or Week pushed from the hub, Not played yet's push among them, or Back between stats pages;
  - an album, artist page, bar or sheet opened from one, and Back from it;
  - a tray tap;
  - a minute, a fold, a day's change;
  - the card's return, or a remount (its page offers Try again).
- **The held condition** is `statsview::onStatsPages()` (7.1). `Ui` evaluates it after each navigation, tab change and dark change; its edges call `statsRelease()` and `statsAsk(Visit)`. A wake reaches the ask through the dark change's edge, not through `enter()`.
- **Nothing patches a page.** A page shows its lists as of the ask. A play counted while a stats page is open changes no row; the next visit's ask has it.

*As decided (2026-11-21, later; "Borrow Dance's block"; V8):* the life above stands, but for these changes.
- **Lent, not carved.** At a visit ask's turn, before the take, if none is held, the snapshot is the Dance tab's 4,096 B tracker scratch, through `statshold::borrow()` (main.cpp) and `DanceMode::lendScratch()`. The loan is refused while the Dance tab is up (shown and lit: the dark turns `DanceMode` off, `src/ui/Ui.cpp:2364-2366`, and the wake on the tab back on, `:2390`), past 18,432 albums (`kBound`), or with no block made at the boot (no PSRAM for the dancer). Each refusal ends the visit NoBlock, No room, its cause on its line.
- **Released, not freed.** Each point above drops the pointer and moves the version; nothing goes back to the heap (`freeSnap()`).
- **The Dance tab coming up** is a release point. `DanceMode::setActive(true)` calls `dancelend::reclaim()`, `statsRelease("the Dance tab")`, before its tracker reads into the block (`src/app/DanceMode.cpp:80`), from the tab's `enter()` (`src/ui/DancePage.cpp:45`) and the wake on the tab (`src/ui/Ui.cpp:2390`). An ask in flight then:
  - a page's ask is cancelled with it (`release()` is `cancel()` and the release);
  - a console visit (`Hv`, `Hv1`, `Hu`) goes on (its rank and walk work in its own block, and the walk never reads the snapshot) and ends Cancelled with nothing written: at `finishVisit()`, before the copy-out, or at its landing, before `resolve()` ("its snapshot released meanwhile (the Dance tab came up, or the fence): nothing written": `src/app/StatsLists.cpp:1198-1202`, `:1406-1410`); one still in Asked meets the refused loan at its turn (No room);
  - a console lean ask (`Hn`, `Hn1`) needs no snapshot, and lands counting from its walk's bits.

  Nothing is asked again: `Hu`'s isn't (only the fence asks `Hu` again), and a page asks once it shows again, with the tab down.
- **A stats page and the Dance tab never show at once,** so a page meets the Dance tab only at its edges (7.1), and only a console ask can be refused for it.

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):*
- **S3a's own frees** (releases since V8):
  - at the fence (`drop()`);
  - at the orderly end (`PlayHistory::flush()` calls `StatsLists::ending()`, plays pending or not);
  - with the card away or held, and with the stats off;
  - at a remount's key: `StatsLists::loop()` checks it each pass, not at a page's `update()`, and not while a visit's ask is under way, since that ask rewrites the snapshot over the index it took;
  - since V8, when the Dance tab comes up (`dancelend::reclaim()`).
- **The frees a navigation makes** (leaving, another tab, the dark, a Live or a rebuild, `browse`) are calls to `release()`, which S3b's `Ui` makes.
- **A held snapshot short of the library's albums** is carved again at the next visit's turn. *Since V8:* it is released there, and the loan, the block whole, is then refused: that is only past 18,432 albums (No room).
- **A console visit** (`Hv`, `Hv1`) frees at its landing the snapshot it carved. (Since V8: releases the snapshot it borrowed.)
- **`Hu`'s** snapshot is kept: `kFake`, set only when `Hu`'s own ask carved it. (Since V8: borrowed it.)
- **A refused visit** frees the snapshot it carved when every grade is refused (since V8, releases the one it borrowed).
- **A visit whose loan would be refused** (V8's review) ends before `stats.bin`'s directory is read, when no snapshot is held (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:667-676`). One held and short of the index's albums is read first, then released and refused: the check of the review's fixes found the early end covers only a visit with no snapshot held, and the other case practically unreachable (a snapshot held over a library of 18,432 albums or fewer, then a visit's turn over an index of more).

**S3a: the lit hold** (the owner's default (a)).
- **Each UI ask's line** gives:
  - the hold, from the take to the give-back;
  - whether the screen was lit at the take, at the rank's start and at the walk's;
  - the rank's priority;
  - the queued, rank and walk ms, with the passes and the longest;
  - the visit's summed hold over its takes, which is what `kLitCapMs` is set against.
- **`kLitCapMs`** is 3,000, in `StatsLists.cpp`. A visit whose holds pass it is counted and said ("past the 3 s cap").
- **The cap** (the owner's default (a): "S3 caps it"). Its form is S3's default:
  - UI asks, and `Hv`, `Hn` and `Hu`, rank at priority 1 even when lit, as dark: the measured 0.70-1.02 s (9.6). The cost is the loop sharing the CPU with the rank for its step, which `[ui] slow pass` lines in run 3a-1 measure.
  - The walk takes the dark's passes, up to 8 ms, lit or not, still none in a pass that handled input: the measured 0.65-0.90 s.
  - A backstop: when a visit's summed hold reaches `kLitCapMs`, its block is given back at its next point (queued at once, walking at once, on the worker at the step's end). The page then shows Capped, "Waiting for the card…" with Try again, and asks nothing by itself, so a visit can't hold 3 s again and again.
- **Its switch:** S3a builds the cap whole behind `kLitCap` (a constant: off in V1-V7, on since V8) and `Hc0`/`Hc1` (RAM, the session). Run 3a-1 measures every ask with it off and on.
- **After run 3a-1:** if no lit hold passes 3 s with the cap off, `kLitCap` stays off, and the figures are recorded. Otherwise `kLitCap` is set on for S3b, and the figures, the cap's costs and its form go to the owner, who may overrule: another form (the backstop alone, say), or no cap, the lit holds accepted as measured.
- *As run (2026-11-21, run 3a-1; MEASURED):* lit holds passed 3 s with the cap off (each take's 2,681-6,104 ms, the visits' summed lines up to 6,155 ms), so `kLitCap` goes on for S3b, and the figures go to the owner. With the cap on the holds were 1,207-2,379 ms, none capped ("S3a: the device pass", below).
- *As decided (2026-11-21):* the owner kept the cap, "Cap on": `kLitCap` is `true` since V8.

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):*
- **`kLitCap`** is `false` in V1-V7, and `true` since V8 (`src/app/StatsLists.cpp:48`; the owner's "Cap on"); `Hc`'s help says "on by default".
- **The landing's line** gives every figure listed above.
- **A take that ends without landing** gives its own line, `sayTake()` (V6): put back, capped, ended with a reason, or at the fence. The line reads "[listen] X: the take's figures (not landed): …" and gives:
  - the hold and the screen at the take;
  - the rank's priority and the screen at its start, with the queued and rank ms, or "not ranked" with the queued ms;
  - the walk's ms, passes, slices and longest, or "no walk";
  - the loop's pieces so far.

  Run 3a-1 reads put-back and capped takes there.
- **A visit whose holds pass 3 s** is said once ("past the 3 s cap") and counted for the session.
- **The visit's summed hold** is a u16 of ms, saturating.
- **The cap** applies to the lean ask too. Its backstop also acts when a give-back brings the holds to 3 s.
- **The walk's 1 s hold** gives the block back for a list moving, the card's guard, input (lit, or under the cap), Auto DJ's join working, or the UI or index not up.

**S3a: tests.**
- `test_stats_rank`:
  - statsref's lists after the order swap, on every fixture boot and rank scenario;
  - the u64 played set and the old report fields unchanged;
  - totals at and past each cap;
  - the 7 days and the span against statsref: clock set, clock unset, a play across a UTC midnight, DAYS at its 400 rows;
  - `rcntFirstDay` with RCNT capped;
  - the u40 set equal to the u64 set's top 40 bits on every fixture;
  - the null window while undated;
  - the played-set-only rank: no offers, no RCNT or DAYS read, its played set equal to the full rank's on every fixture, clock set and unset (a dated rank with no window room is `Room` today);
  - the overlay case: every region filled with a pattern before the rank, the played set written over the dead window's rows, and the lists byte-equal.
- `test_stats_map`:
  - the u40 album bits equal the u64 bits and statsref's on every fixture;
  - an injected top-40 collision hides exactly its album and never shows a played one (call 3);
  - `Wanted::rowsCap`;
  - `seal()`'s heap sort: the sealed set equal to the insertion sort's on every fixture, reversed input and equal hashes.
- `test_stats_visit`:
  - the layout and bytes (3,860 B at 1,706 albums; the 4,096 B bound and its trim past about 3,200 albums);
  - `write` and `resolve`: orphans, collisions, caps, the totals less the orphans, the indices; a block part-written by an earlier try rewritten whole;
  - the directory and `albumAt`;
  - `when`'s codes;
  - `layout()` against the grade table, every grade, both columns, with and without a clock;
  - `phaseOf()`: every why, every Rank-lane wait and every yield after the take (Working, then Waiting from 3 s, no count), every read fault (Unread) and nothing else Unread, the refusals (NoRoom), the cap (Capped), the card (NoCard).
- `test_scan_scheduler`: `yieldNow()` equal to the wait `decide()` gives a rank in the same `In`, for each wait.
- `test_stats_fold`: `sinceDayNow()` (set, unset, the first save's day) and `liveRound()`.
- `test_autodj_join`, the model's S3 half:
  - **The grades:** `StatsRun::Visit{Full, Lists}` and `StatsRun::NotPlayed{Lean, NoFolders}` at P = 2,214 and 19,478, after the boot's join and after each update's rejoin, with and without the Opus kept. Each grade taken or refused is pinned, and every ask leaves the heap as it found it (`test_an_s2_ask_in_a_model_heap`'s rule).
  - **The held snapshot** (A4): `StatsRun::page{bytes, Plain}` at 512, 3,860 and 4,096 B. It is carved before each step's visit take (after the Live and the rejoin), held through that step's MP3, FLAC or Opus opens, its `Dc`, the records' save and the next memory check (`alsoFreed` counts it at the fence), and freed at the next fence. Its steps are ways of `test_the_listening_stats_across_updates` (V5), so the wide build runs them. It is scored by `stepFails` over the 13 named histories (65 steps) and with `-DSTATS_WIDE` (360). Pass: it fails no step the control (main's queue 2,048 B longer: 2 of 360) doesn't. This is a pre-check only: the device decides, to the byte (run 3a-4; 13).
  - **Nothing held** (the control): the S3 asks with no snapshot leave every figure the twin's.
  - **A reduced-room shape** at 286,708 B: Full taken at P = 19,478 with TRKD 1,365 with the snapshot elsewhere, refused with it carved out of that block (Lists, then the lean ask taken); at TRKD's cap, Full and the lean ask refused and the no-folders grade taken.
- `test_stats_store` and `test_stats_file`: unchanged, as the control.

*As built (S3a, 2026-11-21):* the tests as listed, with these differences.
- **`test_stats_rank`**: the u40 set keeps two equal rows for two played hashes sharing their top 40 bits, as the u64 set keeps two, so it equals the u64 set's top 40 bits row for row. Three rank scenarios are new (rank-days: DAYS at its 400 rows; rank-midnight: plays around UTC midnights; rank-rcnt-cap: RCNT capped, its oldest day inside the 30), eight in all. rank-big's description is corrected to its 635 base rows: it said 619, and its rows didn't change.
- **`test_stats_map`**: `seal()` is pinned against S2's insertion sort, kept in the test as the reference.
- **`test_stats_visit`** has eight cases:
  - it checks `layout()` against the grade table at the table's 1,488 B head; the model takes the firmware's 1,600 B;
  - it runs the visit's whole way on the 31 fixture boots;
  - it checks `phaseOf()` exhaustively, `served` and `CardAway` among the inputs;
  - since cf72100, `test_the_worker_s_give_up_holds_30_s_then_the_take` runs StatsLists' pass order over the stall's rules. The ask is held 30 s and taken at the first pass at or past 30 s (10 ms and 37 ms passes, `millis()` wrapping), reading Waiting from 3 s; a Rank-lane wait, S2's join gate or the write gate across the 30 s mark takes it at its first clear pass; the rule before the fix takes none in 60,000 passes;
  - since b16a189 (the device pass's fix), a ninth: `test_elapsed_ms_with_a_start_ahead_of_now`.
- **`test_scan_scheduler`**: `test_yield_now_is_a_ranks_wait`.
- **`test_autodj_join`** has 48 cases.
  - `test_an_s3_ask_in_a_model_heap` has the grades and nothing held. Since V6 (finding 10) it also has a page's 3,860 B snapshot carved before each visit's take and held, at P = 2,214 and P = 19,478, through the second update's memory check (the join given back) and its rejoin. It pins against the visits alone: Full, Full, Full; three snapshots freed (two fences, the end); the boot's join 589,812 B, joined 360,436 B, the check's give-back 360,436 B; the boot's lowest 168,136 B and the rejoins' 446,796 B (MODELLED). The model's memory check gives back the join alone, so main.cpp's `alsoFreed` count of the snapshot isn't modelled.
  - `test_an_s3_ask_in_a_reduced_room` asks each grade alone, with the snapshot held (fc5bf26), not only a page's chain.
  - A4's ways and the fallback's are ways of `test_the_listening_stats_across_updates`. Their snapshot is carved before each step's visit take and after the boot's join (held to update 0's fence), with the S3 asks right after each Live and rejoin. The histories have no MP3 start and no memory check.

*As built (V8, V9 and V8's review, 2026-11-21):*
- **`test_stats_visit`**, ten cases: `test_the_dance_tab_s_block_lent` pins `loanOk()` (lent with the block made and the tab down, up to `kBound`; never with the tab up or no block; every library to 18,432 albums lent, 18,433 not), and `phaseOf()`'s loops run every why to 255, 128 among them (Working at 2,999 ms, Waiting at 3,000; the boot's and the card's rules over it; `waitStartMoves(128)` false; `elapsedMs()`).
- **`test_card_jobs`**, 19 cases. `test_the_scans_memory_held`: `scanHeld()` false after `begin()` and through the walk and its compactions; true from the first Scan `prepare()`, through a compaction run mid-rest, a trim mid-rest and the rest's end, until a trim after it; false after that trim. The playing track's scan holds it while its chunk waits, until `prepareUpdate()` and a trim; a Rescan's rest holds it from its first step. `test_a_card_that_refuses` pins `scanStuck()`: true for a chunk the card refused after a failed compaction, false while a compaction is asked (`askCompact()`), true again when that fails.
- **`test_autodj_join`**, 48 cases. The borrowed way (V9: `StatsRun::Carve::Borrowed`, the yard `kBorrowed`): a visit after the boot's join and each update's rejoin, its snapshot lent (`loanOk()`, the tab down in the model) and nothing carved. Pinned in all three builds: `same(kRework, kBorrowed)` and `same(kVisits, kBorrowed)`, no figure moved, the failing steps the visits alone's, their grades, asks and refusals turn by turn, every turn lent and none carved. The model has no tag scan's memory, so its visits don't wait out a rest; the device's may come later, `Hu`'s re-ask after a fence among them (a comment, V8's review). Its turn reads `stats.bin`'s directory before the loan, where the firmware checks the loan first; no yard reaches a refused loan, so no figure differs (the check of the review's fixes).

*As built (V10b, 86f1990, 2026-11-22; "Don't wait at the floor"):*
- **`test_stats_visit`**, eleven cases. `test_the_rest_gate_s_word` pins `restHolds()`'s whole truth table (and at compile time): held only with the memory carved, not stuck, and the floor not holding the scan. Then with a `ScanScheduler`'s own `batteryLow()`, the memory held: the word true at 60 % and 10 %, false at 9 % off USB, kept false at 15 % and with no reading, true again at 16 %, false at 5 % off USB, true at 5 % and with no reading on USB.
- **`test_scan_scheduler`**, 30 cases. `test_the_battery_floor` asserts `batteryLow()` at every pass: false with no reading at the start and at 10 %; true below 10 %, kept through 9, 12, 15, a reading not known, 10 and 15; false above 15 %, then with no reading, and on USB at 5 %; true unplugged at 5 %; false plugged, and on USB with no reading.

**S3a: device runs.** These are console only. The setup:
- the spare as S2's pass left it (its stats are test data), with silent mode (`z`) after every boot, but in run 3a-7;
- main de4f52f's CI build as the control, in the same scripts;
- the lists' expectations are statsref's over the card's copies, made after the runs.

The commands are those S2's pass used (`H…`, `D`, `Dc`, `gb`, `qp<n>`, `s`, `z`), `Hc0`/`Hc1` (RAM), plus three `ui` lines that change nothing saved:
- `uiv` wakes the screen (the wake at `src/ui/Ui.cpp:2948`, before any letter) and prints the panel's scan and nothing else (`:2980-2992`);
- `ui1` shows the Library tab (`tapTab()`, `:2949-2950`: navigation only), so a swipe lands on Artists' list, not on Now Playing's seek bar, where a drag ends in `seekTo()` (`src/ui/NowPlayingPage.cpp:1248-1256`);
- `uis<x0>,<y0>,<x1>,<y1>,<ms>`, the scripted swipe (its help at `src/main.cpp:1528-1537`, its parser at `:1538-1555`, RAM): here `uis160,200,160,60,300`, inside the list (y under 240; from 240 down is the button strip). A bare or short `uis` only prints the help.

Never sent:
- `uiL<tag>`, which writes NVS (`src/app/UiLang.cpp:50-56`);
- `@time`, which writes the RTC (`src/app/BoardClock.cpp:59`, `:332`);
- `f`, `B`, `P`, `c`, and any line starting with `-` or `!`;
- `@format`;
- `D1`, and any settings, volume, Bluetooth pair or forget, or power command.

A bare `ui` line doesn't wake the screen: it prints the state and returns (`Ui.cpp:2940-2943`). A UTC midnight and the languages are host tests instead (`test_stats_rank`'s days, `test_ui_library`).
